===== /Users/dbox/repos/wt/vickie/bdp-integration-logs/transaction-sequence-member-plan-v5-B-preparation-main9a42/actual-review/source-dispositions.md SHA256 e8b00531af0513090f2cdb9f2af90d0d9baad040e8abfc32525a7d6e98c1495c =====
# Source dispositions of actual focused B review

Root owns final acceptance. Read-only source audit on committed9a42, using git show rather than active TX draft. Actual stdout d29e8dcbc778f52ccda1d45c52c6897773514f27a35340f0f7be2228133728fc is retained unfiltered. Its opening summary says3blockers+6material+6minor, but the body enumerates18items: B1-1..7, B2-1..6, B3-1..5. All18 are addressed below. No code/plan edits, imports, tests, services or retry.

**Recommended disposition:** retain accepted A19 semantics and all existing oracles/budgets. Clarify exact append position and its tail-removal reconstruction, callback/writer mechanics and the complete deadline inventory. The three alleged blockers do not demonstrate an unimplementable witness: two rest on false source premises and the last is a concrete preservation detail to specify before drafting. No public qualification follows.

All app/cumulative line references below are committed9a42. Future authoring requires actual PR129 and parameter-context merged-parent reanchor, not only this historical review base.

## B1 — all seven findings

**B1-1: reject blocker; optional explicit ordinal wording.** HTTP transaction-http.ts294–296 rejects any outer idempotency-key on sequence. transaction-store.ts1784–1810 classifies/adopts each member.key and nextSequence1849ff replays retained state; transaction-sequence.ts32–76 projects retained entries under current envelope positions. Sequence and replay intentionally have identical bodies/member keys, no outer header. Existing minimumRequests at2260–2328 already has two header-keyless sequence requests with member key minimum-c, and replay ledger2243–2247 includes m-replay-c→m-create-c whole-json. The suggested header key or weakened non-equality oracle would be wrong. v5 explicitly says identical replay is recognized only at its later exact ledger position; position is an independent state discriminator even when wire bytes match. Restate a literal next-index ledger if useful; do not infer a need for a new key.

**B1-2: reject blocker; preserve the early assertion.** crossCarrierWire.finish at1976–1981 only reads active/facts and performs assertions; it neither mutates state nor depends on observe returning. The fact current callers invoke it after await does not prohibit a wrapper calling it earlier at a checked boundary and again at the end. Require cross.finish() before first new sequence handoff, while retaining terminal finish. No new completed() API is necessary.

**B1-3: reject proposed broad sentinel relaxation; clarify composition and actual order.** Actual catalog sequence.member-transactions is index55, after failed-retained50/cross-carrier46. Existing selected TX rows end in failed-retained at manifest index13; new selected row is append index14 under the already-required catalog order (RU inherited minimum remains first). Thus it follows the cross row in all three full traversals, and the handoff is reachable. A small app-local wrapper can call minimum for every response, route only exact new-row ledger positions to the member observer, and forward every other response to the unchanged cross sentinel. Current flat composition is not an interface restriction. Do not replace the broad old sentinel with a blanket body-digest exemption, which would weaken unrelated URL detection. Pin append index14 before drafting.

**B1-4: accept optional cheap static disjointness pin, not an observed collision.** minimumRequests2260–2328 creates one beads/c member with minimum-c/title minimum-c. V5's414B request has three members a/missing/b and member-a/member-fail/member-b. Their actual UTF8 hashes differ; the new keys neither start minimum- nor equal carrier-create/carrier-update. Existing sentinel2358–2366 therefore remains compatible. Freeze these facts in the existing app static callback if root accepts; no extra callback/HTTP.

**B1-5: premise incomplete; retain exact helper evidence.** The plan already requires all three distinct named client actions, no executionError, original sourceReceiptObservation.action/pointer/root/operator/digest/session, internal status/request/body successes and helper full-original equality (transactional-observation.ts255–309). That is stronger than merely counting a prefix skip; missing or wrong receipt reads are not visible only through aggregate213. For clearer implementation, an app ledger may record three exact already-authorized roots at their expected client positions and finish7raw+3client, but never grant authority to arbitrary startsWith(receipts/) URLs. No new transport/runner API or weaker helper binding.

**B1-6: accept wording correction.** afterExchange returns Promise<void> (app114) and its return is discarded at301. Say observe-and-assert only; it cannot replace the response. Do not implement a fictitious response-return seam.

**B1-7: retain pre-cleanup external report, clarify callback placement.** Actual observe assigns the genuine result then invokes onObserved at326–327 before cleanup339ff. The existing positive writer already uses this callback. Capturing/writing there preserves evidence even if await later throws, without requiring postchecks to execute after cleanup failure. Recommend explicitly reuse onObserved for new negative and affected existing cross-control writers (current cross writer is after await2217–2221), and preserve any callback error as primary while finally runs. Do not drop retention or suppress cleanup failure. No new report-body inference.

## B2 — all six findings

**B2-1: accept concrete tail-format preparation correction; not a need for broad refactor.** The new row's catalog-compatible location is append index14. Existing minimum/cross removals2408–2415/856–868 target non-final rows and may remain unchanged after first removing the new final row. Specify exact removal of comma + newline + four spaces + raw new object, assert it changes the text, then require byte-identical whole immediate-parent manifest/coverage reconstruction. This restores the former final row's missing comma exactly. The new removal must not copy a non-final trailing-comma pattern blindly. Existing28rawobject/full-parent checks remain independent. One narrow tail layer is enough; no generic two-form helper is required when index14 is explicitly fixed.

**B2-2: close by source.** assertOriginalObjects828–835 iterates known id/hash/byte triples and looks them up in objectSlices; it does not assert map/set cardinality. Extra new rows are tolerated while existing bytes remain checked. Keep it unchanged; do not extend historical pinned sets or weaken comparisons. Separate28immediate-parent proof is still required.

**B2-3: close with explicit append invariant.** Actual catalog order puts the new selected row last. Every used route already appears earlier, so first-use15method+URL order and digest remain unchanged. State append index14 and independently recompute the route order/digest during draft measurement. No alternate route-order amendment or weaker set-only check is needed on this plan. A changed actual parent could require re-disposition; do not preauthorize changing its route digest.

**B2-4: reject density assumption/automatic ceiling reduction.** The proposed bounds are simultaneous stop ceilings, not a promise that260assertions fit every byte/node cap. V5 already says stop before tests and obtain root's measured allowance decision; forbids minification/dropped oracles. No row is authored, so there is no demonstrated overrun. Make the conjunction explicit if useful. Do not assume command-minimum density or weaken proof to an invented196assertion budget. Actual canonical and all41mutated specimens remain mandatory before any fence amendment.

**B2-5: accept small label-accounting precision.** The positive title353 and summary kind589/comment588 should describe15rows after the draft changes. Those edits are in the already-owned app path; this is not a fifth path/scope expansion. Preserve historical fourteen logs and exact execution selector changes in the measured packet.

**B2-6: accept explicit callback inventory wording.** The historical reconstruction/order/action/fence callback836 and pagination callback1007 are both already in the eight selections. Per-row action-count map892–907 also needs the new10-action row. With append14, existing pagination stays index10; preserve that exact positional+semantic oracle rather than gratuitously changing it. No new callback/full-file execution.

## B3 — all five findings

**B3-1: accept deadline-inventory precision; reject automatic timeout increase.** The review correctly identifies global receipt denial at980–996 with30s, but its “every other full traversal120s” premise is false: ordinary Resource capture838–850 and invalid Resource-as-receipt852–885 are also30s and both traverse the new row. Their new work is10HTTP/1child each; global denial adds3HTTP/1child. Other unchanged startup tests have30s and cancellation tests20s but do not gain a sequence traversal. Retained exact PR128 log67cc58ff has those three durations19388ms(line5073),20761ms(5377),14010ms(5578), leaving observed10612/9239/15990ms respectively. These are samples, not guaranteed headroom. Retain30s/20s/120s as actually defined; no blanket statement that all cohorts have120s. If measured draft/current CI shows insufficient capacity, stop/report root rather than automatically raising30→120 or narrowing the global-denial selection. Existing four local cohorts remain unchanged.

**B3-2: accept existing datum.** PR128 log2738 records installed-tx-fourteen-partial-summary elapsedMs20839.126314,22launched/closed children,21sessions on tested synthetic062fd108 (same9a42tree). Compared with63000, observed margin42160.873686ms. Current whole job margin201s remains separate and is not a per-case bound. Keep63000 unchanged; no new measurement run required just to obtain this historical sample.

**B3-3: close; already explicit.** V5 ten-action table rows35–44 and the focused prompt's ordered list put first helper GET at action3 following sequence/recoveryA. Preserve this literal order in the draft. The3HTTP denial increment is not unconstrained. No budget change.

**B3-4: accept terminology precision.** Mutation40/unchanged14 is orthogonal to reachability40full/5ready/1global-denial/2selected/6abort-preflight. The40full bucket includes unchanged fullpositive and excludes mutated preflight failure; do not equate the two axes. Both sets already have expanded source inventory; label them separately without changing totals.

**B3-5: decline invented1100s gate; preserve existing hard stop/report.** Existing plan says defer if capacity insufficient, no job/test/runtime increase. Root owns future execution and exact CI admission. Adding arbitrary1100s now has no measured normative basis. Retain current1200s job,63000positive and actual callback limits; report unexpected failures with no automatic retry or budget increase.

+    missing = { operation: "deleteBead", bead: "beads/missing" };
+  const sequence = {
+    operations: [
+      { ...first, idempotencyKey: "member-a" },
+      { ...missing, ...(negative ? { bead: "beads/a" } : {}), idempotencyKey: "member-fail" },
+      { ...last, idempotencyKey: "member-b" },
+    ],
+  };
+  const post = (route: string, body: unknown, key?: string) => ({
+    url: `${scope}operations/${route}`,
+    body,
+    headers: [
+      { name: "accept", value: "application/json" },
+      { name: "content-type", value: "application/json" },
+      ...(key === undefined ? [] : [{ name: "idempotency-key", value: key }]),
+    ],
+  });
+  return [
+    post("sequence", sequence),
+    post("batch", { operations: [first] }, "member-a"),
+    post("batch", { operations: [missing] }, "member-fail"),
+    post("batch", { operations: [last] }, "member-b"),
+    post("sequence", sequence),
+    {
+      url: `${scope}beads/?limit=32`,
+      body: undefined,
+      headers: [{ name: "accept", value: "application/json" }],
+    },
+    {
+      url: `${scope}events/`,
+      body: undefined,
+      headers: [{ name: "accept", value: "application/json" }],
+    },
+  ];
+}
+const memberFailureIds = [
+  "serving-position",
+  "middle-type",
+  "middle-code",
+  "middle-status",
+  "middle-retry",
+  "middle-no-outcome",
+  "middle-no-deleted",
+];
+function memberHandoff(
+  index: number,
+  url: string,
+  digest: string | undefined,
+  key: string | undefined,
+  writeState: string | undefined,
+) {
+  return (
+    (index === 0 || index === 4) &&
+    url === `${scope}operations/sequence` &&
+    digest === "f3cde631127aaf530ea5349330e6c7bb46766b02b135f9c7c59acfcd2f2c71c7" &&
+    key === undefined &&
+    writeState === "complete"
+  );
+}
+/** Full actual values stay local to assertions. Reports retain only identities,
+ * digests and performed-pair inventory; receipt helper equality is separate. */
+function sequenceMemberWire(negative = false) {
+  assertMemberReplayLedger(memberReplayLedger);
+  const validator = createJsonSchemaValidator(schema),
+    requests = memberRequests(negative);
+  const observedIds: string[] = [],
+    performed: string[][] = [],
+    resources: Record<string, unknown>[] = [],
+    receipts: Record<string, unknown>[] = [],
+    receiptFacts: { action: string; root: string; digest: string }[] = [];
+  let initial: unknown, epoch: string | undefined, view: string | undefined;
+  const problem = (index: number) => ({
+    type: "https://github.com/gastownhall/bdp/problems/not-found",
+    code: "resource-not-found",
+    status: 404,
+    retry: "after-state-change",
+    operationIndex: index,
+  });
+  function resource(actual: Record<string, unknown>, name: string, title: string) {
+    expect(typeof actual.revision).toBe("string");
+    expect((actual.revision as string).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    expect(actual).toEqual({
+      id: `${scope}beads/${name}`,
+      type: `${scope}types/task`,
+      properties: { title },
+      metadata: {},
+      revision: actual.revision,
+    });
+  }
+  return {
+    count: () => observedIds.length,
+    async afterExchange(response: HttpExchangeResponse) {
+      const index = observedIds.length,
+        id = memberRawIds[index]!,
+        expected = requests[index]!;
+      expect(index).toBeLessThan(negative ? 1 : 7);
+      expect(response.url).toBe(expected.url);
+      const request = response.exactRequest!;
+      expect(request.source).toBe("raw-http1-serializer");
+      expect(request.writeState).toBe("complete");
+      expect(request.bodyPresent).toBe(expected.body !== undefined);
+      // Check names before values so an unexpected credential cannot appear in diagnostics.
+      expect(request.headerLines.slice(0, expected.headers.length).map((h) => h.name)).toEqual(
+        expected.headers.map((h) => h.name),
+      );
+      expect(request.headerLines.slice(0, expected.headers.length)).toEqual(expected.headers);
+      expect(
+        request.headerLines
+          .slice(expected.headers.length)
+          .some((h) => h.name.toLowerCase().startsWith("bdp-")),
+      ).toBe(false);
+      expect(request.bodyDigest).toBe(
+        expected.body === undefined
+          ? undefined
+          : createHash("sha256").update(JSON.stringify(expected.body)).digest("hex"),
+      );
+      expect(response.status).toBe(200);
+      expect(response.headers["content-type"]).toBe("application/json");
+      const body = JSON.parse(response.bodyText);
+      expect(
+        validator.validate(
+          `#/$defs/${index === 0 || index === 4 ? "transactionalSequenceResponse" : index < 4 ? "mutationReceipt" : index === 5 ? "currentBeadCollection" : "eventPage"}`,
+          body,
+        ),
+      ).toEqual([]);
+      const actualEpoch = response.headers["bdp-scope-epoch"],
+        actualView = response.headers["bdp-authorization-view"];
+      expect(typeof actualEpoch).toBe("string");
+      expect(actualEpoch?.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+      expect(typeof actualView).toBe("string");
+      expect(actualView?.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+      expect(response.headers["bdp-scope-position"]).toBe(negative ? "pos-3" : "pos-2");
+      if (epoch !== undefined) {
+        expect(actualEpoch).toBe(epoch);
+        expect(actualView).toBe(view);
+      }
+      if (index === 0 || index === 4) {
+        expect(Object.keys(body)).toEqual(["results"]);
+        expect(body.results).toHaveLength(3);
+        resource(body.results[0].resource, "a", "member-first");
+        resource(body.results[2].resource, "b", "member-last");
+        for (const i of [0, 2])
+          expect(body.results[i]).toEqual({
+            operationIndex: i,
+            outcome: "created",
+            resource: body.results[i].resource,
+          });
+        if (negative) {
+          expect(body.results[1]).toEqual({
+            operationIndex: 1,
+            outcome: "deleted",
+            deleted: {
+              resourceKind: "bead",
+              resource: {
+                id: `${scope}beads/a`,
+                type: `${scope}types/task`,
+                revision: body.results[0].resource.revision,
+              },
+            },
+          });
+          expect(body.results[1].deleted.resource).not.toHaveProperty("properties");
+          expect(body.results[1].deleted.resource).not.toHaveProperty("metadata");
+        } else expect(body.results[1]).toEqual(problem(1));
+        const pair = memberReplayLedger.find(([replay]) => replay === id);
+        if (pair) {
+          expect(initial).toBeDefined();
+          expect(body).toEqual(initial);
+          performed.push([...pair]);
+        } else {
+          initial = structuredClone(body);
+          resources.push(
+            structuredClone(body.results[0].resource),
+            structuredClone(body.results[2].resource),
+          );
+        }
+      } else if (index < 4) {
+        const failed = index === 2,
+          position = index === 3 ? "pos-2" : "pos-1",
+          key = ["member-a", "member-fail", "member-b"][index - 1];
+        expect(body).toMatchObject({
+          status: failed ? "failed" : "completed",
+          detail: "available",
+          idempotencyKey: key,
+          scopeEpoch: actualEpoch,
+          authorizationView: actualView,
+          requiredPosition: position,
+        });
+        expect(typeof body.id).toBe("string");
+        expect(body.id.startsWith(`${scope}receipts/`)).toBe(true);
+        expect(body.id.slice(`${scope}receipts/`.length)).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/);
+        expect(new URL(body.id).href).toBe(body.id);
+        expect(typeof body.transaction).toBe("string");
+        expect(body.transaction.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+        expect(typeof body.expiresAt).toBe("string");
+        expect(Number.isFinite(Date.parse(body.expiresAt))).toBe(true);
+        if (failed) {
+          expect(body.problem).toEqual(problem(0));
+          for (const field of ["effectPosition", "results", "next"])
+            expect(body).not.toHaveProperty(field);
+        } else {
+          expect(body.effectPosition).toBe(position);
+          expect(body.next).toBeNull();
+          expect(body.results).toEqual([
+            { operationIndex: 0, outcome: "created", resource: resources[index === 1 ? 0 : 1] },
+          ]);
+          expect(body).not.toHaveProperty("problem");
+        }
+        receipts.push(structuredClone(body));
+        expect(response.wireResponseBytes).toBeDefined();
+        receiptFacts.push({
+          action: id,
+          root: body.id,
+          digest: createHash("sha256").update(response.wireResponseBytes!).digest("hex"),
+        });
+      } else if (index === 5) {
+        expect(body.items).toEqual(resources);
+        expect(body.next).toBeNull();
+      } else {
+        expect(body.events).toHaveLength(2);
+        expect(body.next).toBeNull();
+        const identity = [
+          ["epoch", actualEpoch],
+          ["view", actualView],
+          ["source", `${scope}events/`],
+        ]
+          .map((tagged) => createHash("sha256").update(JSON.stringify(tagged)).digest("base64url"))
+          .join("_");
+        for (const [i, name] of ["a", "b"].entries()) {
+          const event = body.events[i],
+            record = resources[i]!;
+          expect(typeof event.time).toBe("string");
+          expect(Number.isFinite(Date.parse(event.time))).toBe(true);
+          expect(event).toEqual({
+            id: `ev_${identity}_${i === 0 ? "pos-1" : "pos-2"}_0`,
+            source: `${scope}events/`,
+            type: "created",
+            subject: `${scope}beads/${name}`,
+            subjectType: `${scope}types/task`,
+            ordinal: 0,
+            time: event.time,
+            transaction: receipts[i === 0 ? 0 : 2]!.transaction,
+            data: {
+              revision: record.revision,
+              properties: record.properties,
+              metadata: record.metadata,
+            },
+          });
+        }
+      }
+      epoch = actualEpoch;
+      view = actualView;
+      observedIds.push(id);
+    },
+    summary: () => ({
+      kind: "sequence-member-wire-supplement",
+      negative,
+      observedIds: [...observedIds],
+      performedComparisons: performed.map((p) => [...p]),
+      receiptFacts: receiptFacts.map((f) => ({ ...f })),
+    }),
+    finish() {
+      expect(observedIds).toEqual(negative ? ["s-sequence"] : memberRawIds);
+      expect(performed).toEqual(negative ? [] : memberReplayLedger);
+      if (!negative) {
+        expect(new Set(receipts.map((r) => r.id)).size).toBe(3);
+        expect(new Set(receipts.map((r) => r.transaction)).size).toBe(3);
+      }
+    },
+  };
+}
+function composedMemberWire(
+  cross: ReturnType<typeof crossCarrierWire>,
+  minimum: ReturnType<typeof commandMinimumWire>,
+  member: ReturnType<typeof sequenceMemberWire>,
+) {
+  let handedOff = false;
+  return async (response: HttpExchangeResponse) => {
+    await minimum.afterExchange(response);
+    const request = response.exactRequest,
+      key = request?.headerLines.find((h) => h.name.toLowerCase() === "idempotency-key")?.value;
+    const exempt = memberHandoff(
+      member.count(),
+      response.url,
+      request?.bodyDigest,
+      key,
+      request?.writeState,
+    );
+    if (exempt && !handedOff) {
+      cross.finish();
+      handedOff = true;
+    }
+    if (!exempt) await cross.afterExchange(response);
+    if (handedOff) await member.afterExchange(response);
+  };
+}
+function assertMemberPositive(
+  observed: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof observe>>,
+  wire: ReturnType<typeof sequenceMemberWire>,
+) {
+  const row = observed.result.scenarios.find((r) => r.id === memberId)!,
+    declared = bundle.manifest.scenarios.find((r) => r.id === memberId)!;
+  expect(row.state).toBe("pass");
+  expect(row.cleanupError).toBeUndefined();
+  expect(row.prerequisiteFailure).toBeUndefined();
+  expect(row.exchanges.map((e) => e.request.id)).toEqual(memberActionIds);
+  expect(row.exchanges.map((e) => e.request.method)).toEqual([
+    "POST",
+    "POST",
+    "GET",
+    "POST",
+    "GET",
+    "POST",
+    "GET",
+    "POST",
+    "GET",
+    "GET",
+  ]);
+  expect(row.actions!.map((a) => a.action.id)).toEqual([
+    "s-first-facts",
+    "s-failed-facts",
+    "s-last-facts",
+  ]);
+  for (const exchange of row.exchanges) {
+    expect(exchange.transportError).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(exchange.harnessError).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(exchange.assertions.every((a) => a.passed)).toBe(true);
+    expect(exchange.request.exact?.writeState).toBe("complete");
+    if (!exchange.request.id.endsWith("-facts"))
+      expect(exchange.assertions.map((a) => a.id)).toEqual(
+        declared.actions!.find((a) => a.id === exchange.request.id)!.assertions.map((a) => a.id),
+      );
+  }
+  for (const [i, index] of [2, 4, 6].entries()) {
+    const exchange = row.exchanges[index]!,
+      action = row.actions![i]!,
+      fact = wire.summary().receiptFacts[i]!;
+    expect(action.assertions.map((a) => a.id)).toEqual(
+      declared.actions!.find((a) => a.id === action.action.id)!.assertions.map((a) => a.id),
+    );
+    expect(action.assertions.every((a) => a.passed)).toBe(true);
+    expect(action.executionError).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(action.output).toEqual({ kind: "json" });
+    expect(exchange.sourceReceiptObservation).toMatchObject({
+      action: fact.action,
+      pointer: "/id",
+      receipt: fact.root,
+      responseDigest: fact.digest,
+      credentialRef: "operator",
+    });
+    expect(exchange.request.url).toBe(fact.root);
+    expect(exchange.request.exact?.bodyPresent).toBe(false);
+    expect(exchange.dynamicExactConfiguration).toMatchObject({
+      scope,
+      profile: "transactional",
+      routes: [{ method: "GET", url: fact.root }],
+    });
+    expect(exchange.servedSession).toEqual(exchange.sourceReceiptObservation!.session);
+    expect(exchange.assertions.map((a) => [a.id, a.passed])).toEqual([
+      ["actual-status", true],
+      ["actual-request", true],
+      ["actual-body", true],
+    ]);
+  }
+  wire.finish();
+}
+function applyMemberControl(value: CrossCarrierManifest) {
+  const row = value.scenarios.find((r) => r.id === memberId)!;
+  (row.actions[0]!.raw as { body: { value: string } }).body.value = JSON.stringify(
+    memberRequests(true)[0]!.body,
+  );
+}
+function assertMemberControl() {
+  const value = JSON.parse(manifestSource.bytes.toString()) as CrossCarrierManifest,
+    original = structuredClone(value);
+  applyMemberControl(value);
+  expect(value.scenarios.find((r) => r.id === memberId)!.actions).toHaveLength(10);
+  const changed = value.scenarios.find((r) => r.id === memberId)!.actions[0]!;
+  expect((changed.raw as { body: { value: string } }).body.value).toBe(
+    JSON.stringify(memberRequests(true)[0]!.body),
+  );
+  (changed.raw as { body: { value: string } }).body.value = JSON.stringify(
+    memberRequests()[0]!.body,
+  );
+  expect(value).toEqual(original);
+  applyMemberControl(value);
+  expect(() =>
+    createCumulativeTransactionalArtifactBundle(loadProfileContractSelection("transactional"), {
+      manifest: { bytes: Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(value)) },
+      fixture: fixtureSource,
+    }),
+  ).not.toThrow();
+}
+it("discriminates sequence member transactions against a successful middle delete", async () => {
+  const children: ChildProcess[] = [],
+    wire = sequenceMemberWire(true);
+  const observed = await observe({
+    children,
+    scenarioSelection: [memberId],
+    manifest: applyMemberControl,
+    afterExchange: wire.afterExchange,
+    onObserved(observed) {
+      if (process.env.BDP_TRANSACTIONAL_SEQUENCE_MEMBER_CONTROL_OUTPUT)
+        writeFileSync(
+          process.env.BDP_TRANSACTIONAL_SEQUENCE_MEMBER_CONTROL_OUTPUT,
+          JSON.stringify(
+            { sourceCommit, entry, ...observed, supplemental: wire.summary() },
+            null,
+            2,
+          ) + "\n",
+        );
+    },
+  });
+  expect(observed.result.selectedScenarioIds).toEqual([memberId]);
+  expect(observed.result.claimEligible).toBe(false);
+  expect(observed.result.scenarios).toHaveLength(1);
+  const row = observed.result.scenarios[0]!,
+    exchange = row.exchanges[0]!;
+  expect(row.state).toBe("fail");
+  expect(row.cleanupError).toBeUndefined();
+  expect(row.prerequisiteFailure).toBeUndefined();
+  expect(row.actions ?? []).toHaveLength(0);
+  expect(row.exchanges).toHaveLength(1);
+  expect(exchange.request.id).toBe("s-sequence");
+  expect(exchange.request.method).toBe("POST");
+  expect(exchange.request.exact?.writeState).toBe("complete");
+  expect(exchange.response?.status).toBe(200);
+  expect(exchange.transportError).toBeUndefined();
+  expect(exchange.harnessError).toBeUndefined();
+  expect(exchange.assertions.map((a) => a.id)).toEqual(
+    bundle.manifest.scenarios
+      .find((r) => r.id === memberId)!
+      .actions![0]!.assertions.map((a) => a.id),
+  );
+  expect(exchange.assertions.filter((a) => !a.passed).map((a) => a.id)).toEqual(memberFailureIds);
+  for (const id of [
+    "http-status",
+    "schema",
+    "media",
+    "request-headers",
+    "request-body",
+    "middle-no-resource",
+  ])
+    expect(exchange.assertions.find((a) => a.id === id)?.passed).toBe(true);
+  wire.finish();
+  expect(observed.sessions).toHaveLength(1);
+  expect(children).toHaveLength(2);
+  expect(children.every((c) => c.exitCode !== null || c.signalCode !== null)).toBe(true);
+  console.log(
+    JSON.stringify({
+      kind: "installed-tx-sequence-member-control",
+      sourceCommit,
+      exchanges: 1,
+      childLaunches: children.length,
+      closedChildren: children.filter((c) => c.exitCode !== null || c.signalCode !== null).length,
+      failures: exchange.assertions.filter((a) => !a.passed).map((a) => a.id),
+      supplemental: wire.summary(),
+      claimEligible: observed.result.claimEligible,
+    }),
+  );
+}, 120000);

+function withoutSequenceMemberRow(text: string, name: "manifest" | "coverage") {
+  const key = name === "manifest" ? "scenarios" : "rows";
+  const objects = objectSlices(text, key);
+  for (const [id, hash, bytes] of sequenceParentPins[name]) {
+    const raw = objects.get(id)!;
+    expect(Buffer.byteLength(raw), id).toBe(bytes);
+    expect(createHash("sha256").update(raw).digest("hex"), id).toBe(hash);
+  }
+  const raw = objects.get(sequenceMemberId)!;
+  expect(typeof raw).toBe("string");
+  const suffix = `,\n    ${raw}\n  ]\n}\n`;
+  expect(text.endsWith(suffix), "sequence-tail-position").toBe(true);
+  const parent = text.slice(0, -suffix.length) + "\n  ]\n}\n";
+  expect(createHash("sha256").update(parent).digest("hex"), "sequence-full-parent").toBe(
+    name === "manifest"
+      ? "29729151cf6da641545910ec3880f9609c13b319af7bbe99d3d2ad47833dab96"
+      : "4619813e9446e0e714a64c11eac184531950f57720e32c7ef414c7e3342121af",
+  );
+  return parent;
+}
+function assertSequenceMemberObservation(value: typeof manifest) {
+  const row = value.scenarios.find((r: { id: string }) => r.id === sequenceMemberId)!;
+  expect(
+    row.actions.map((a: { id: string }) => a.id),
+    "sequence-action-order",
+  ).toEqual([
+    "s-sequence",
+    "s-first-receipt",
+    "s-first-facts",
+    "s-failed-receipt",
+    "s-failed-facts",
+    "s-last-receipt",
+    "s-last-facts",
+    "s-replay",
+    "s-current",
+    "s-events",
+  ]);
+  const fact = (index: number, id: string) =>
+    row.actions[index].assertions.find((a: { id: string }) => a.id === id);
+  for (const index of [0, 7]) {
+    expect(fact(index, "middle-status"), "sequence-middle-status").toEqual({
+      id: "middle-status",
+      kind: "json-pointer",
+      pointer: "/results/1/status",
+      exists: true,
+      equals: 404,
+    });
+    expect(fact(index, "middle-retry"), "sequence-middle-retry").toEqual({
+      id: "middle-retry",
+      kind: "json-pointer",
+      pointer: "/results/1/retry",
+      exists: true,
+      equals: "after-state-change",
+    });
+  }
+  expect(fact(3, "problem"), "sequence-failed-internal-index").toEqual({
+    id: "problem",
+    kind: "json-pointer",
+    pointer: "/problem",
+    exists: true,
+    equals: {
+      type: "https://github.com/gastownhall/bdp/problems/not-found",
+      code: "resource-not-found",
+      status: 404,
+      retry: "after-state-change",
+      operationIndex: 0,
+    },
+  });
+  expect(row.actions.flatMap((a: { captures: unknown[] }) => a.captures)).toEqual([
+    { binding: "s-first-root", from: { kind: "json-pointer", pointer: "/id" } },
+    { binding: "s-failed-root", from: { kind: "json-pointer", pointer: "/id" } },
+    { binding: "s-last-root", from: { kind: "json-pointer", pointer: "/id" } },
+  ]);
+  expect(row.actions.flatMap((a: { assertions: unknown[] }) => a.assertions)).toHaveLength(209);
+  expect(
+    createHash("sha256").update(JSON.stringify(row)).digest("hex"),
+    "sequence-complete-independent-facts",
+  ).toBe("48e524596890fab7bf578cbbb653ed844178c33b568f8e0c033736d2745598ca");
+}
