/** Private receipt authority: populated only alongside validated actual wire observations. */
import { isDeepStrictEqual } from "node:util";
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import type { ExactHttpConfiguration } from "./raw-http-scenario-target.js";
import type {
  ExactHttpModeOptions,
  RawHttpDialRoute,
  HttpExchangeResponse,
} from "./http-executor.js";
import type { SchemaValidator } from "./schema-validator.js";

export class TransactionalHarnessError extends Error {}
/** Safe target-observation reason, independent of runner implementation. */
export class TransactionalReceiptFailure extends Error {}

export interface TransactionalSessionBinding {
  readonly scenario: string;
  readonly generation: number;
  readonly listenUrl: string;
  readonly runtimeConfigurationIdentity: string;
  readonly configurationDigest: string;
  readonly responseLimits: Readonly<{ headerBytes: 65536; bodyBytes: 1048576; wireBytes: 2097152 }>;
}
export interface TransactionalSession {
  readonly binding: TransactionalSessionBinding;
  readonly dialRoute: RawHttpDialRoute;
  readonly signal: AbortSignal;
  readonly resolveCredentials: ExactHttpModeOptions["resolveCredentials"];
  /** Registers with the same session pending set drained before close/reopen. */
  register<T>(operation: Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
}
export interface TransactionalRunContext {
  current(scenario: string): TransactionalSession;
  reopen(scenario: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
}
interface ReceiptObservationSource {
  readonly action: string;
  readonly receipt: string;
  readonly responseDigest: string;
  readonly credentialRef: string;
  readonly session: TransactionalSessionBinding;
}
/** Old root references retain their exact shape; a page records its own target. */
export type ReceiptObservationReference = ReceiptObservationSource &
  ({ readonly pointer: "/id" } | { readonly pointer: "/next"; readonly page: string });
export interface TransactionalExchangeFields {
  readonly dynamicExactConfiguration?: ExactHttpConfiguration;
  readonly dynamicExactConfigurationDigest?: string;
  readonly sourceReceiptObservation?: ReceiptObservationReference;
  readonly servedSession?: TransactionalSessionBinding;
}
export const TRANSACTIONAL_EXCHANGE_FIELDS = [
  "dynamicExactConfiguration",
  "dynamicExactConfigurationDigest",
  "sourceReceiptObservation",
  "servedSession",
] as const;
export function receiptUrl(value: unknown, scope: string): string {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length > 8192)
    throw new TransactionalHarnessError("unobserved receipt target");
  let url: URL;
  try {
    url = new URL(value);
  } catch (cause) {
    throw new TransactionalHarnessError("unobserved receipt target", { cause });
  }
  const prefix = `${scope}receipts/`;
  if (
    url.href !== value ||
    !value.startsWith(prefix) ||
    !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(value.slice(prefix.length)) ||
    url.search ||
    url.hash ||
    url.username ||
    url.password
  )
    throw new TransactionalHarnessError("unobserved receipt target");
  return value;
}
/** Only a first-page URL actually disclosed by this canonical root is accepted. */
function receiptPageUrl(value: unknown, receipt: string): string {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length > 8192)
    throw new TransactionalHarnessError("unobserved receipt page target");
  let url: URL;
  try {
    url = new URL(value);
  } catch (cause) {
    throw new TransactionalHarnessError("unobserved receipt page target", { cause });
  }
  const prefix = `${receipt}/pages/`;
  if (
    url.href !== value ||
    !value.startsWith(prefix) ||
    !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,256}$/.test(value.slice(prefix.length)) ||
    url.search ||
    url.hash ||
    url.username ||
    url.password
  )
    throw new TransactionalHarnessError("unobserved receipt page target");
  return value;
}
export function createTransactionalObservations(
  context: TransactionalRunContext,
  scenario: string,
  scope: string,
  maximum: number,
) {
  const ledger = new Map<
    string,
    {
      reference: ReceiptObservationReference;
      body: unknown;
      validator: SchemaValidator;
      pageBody?: unknown;
    }
  >();
  let accepted: TransactionalSessionBinding | undefined;
  const session = () => {
    let current: TransactionalSession;
    try {
      current = context.current(scenario);
    } catch (cause) {
      throw new TransactionalHarnessError("transaction session unavailable", { cause });
    }
    const limits = current.binding.responseLimits;
    if (
      limits?.headerBytes !== 65536 ||
      limits.bodyBytes !== 1048576 ||
      limits.wireBytes !== 2097152
    )
      throw new TransactionalHarnessError("transaction session response limits mismatch");
    if (current.signal.aborted || current.binding.scenario !== scenario)
      throw new TransactionalHarnessError("transaction session unavailable");
    if (accepted && !isDeepStrictEqual(current.binding, accepted))
      throw new TransactionalHarnessError("unvalidated physical session transition");
    accepted ??= Object.freeze({ ...current.binding });
    return current;
  };
  return {
    session,
    async reopen(signal: AbortSignal) {
      const previous = session().binding;
      let current: TransactionalSession;
      try {
        await context.reopen(scenario, signal);
        current = context.current(scenario);
      } catch (cause) {
        throw new TransactionalHarnessError("transaction session reopen failed", { cause });
      }
      if (
        current.signal.aborted ||
        current.binding.scenario !== scenario ||
        current.binding.generation <= previous.generation ||
        current.binding.runtimeConfigurationIdentity !== previous.runtimeConfigurationIdentity ||
        current.binding.configurationDigest !== previous.configurationDigest
      )
        throw new TransactionalHarnessError("invalid reopened session");
      accepted = Object.freeze({ ...current.binding });
      session();
    },
    register(
      binding: string,
      pointer: string,
      action: string,
      request: { readonly method: string; readonly url: string },
      response: HttpExchangeResponse,
      value: unknown,
      config: ExactHttpConfiguration,
      credential: string | undefined,
      validator: SchemaValidator | undefined,
      served: TransactionalSessionBinding,
    ) {
      const operation = request.url.startsWith(`${scope}operations/`)
        ? request.url.slice(`${scope}operations/`.length)
        : undefined;
      const mutationSource =
        request.method === "POST" &&
        [
          "create-bead",
          "update-bead",
          "delete-bead",
          "create-link",
          "update-link",
          "delete-link",
          "put-alias",
          "delete-alias",
          "update-where",
          "delete-where",
          "batch",
        ].includes(operation ?? "");
      if ((pointer !== "/id" && pointer !== "/next") || !mutationSource) {
        // Ordinary captures keep their normal meaning. Reusing a binding revokes
        // its prior authority; only canonical mutation responses can grant it.
        ledger.delete(binding);
        return;
      }
      if (response.url !== request.url)
        throw new TransactionalHarnessError("source receipt route mismatch");
      if (
        response.status !== 200 ||
        !response.wireResponseBytes ||
        response.wireResponseBytes.length > 2097152
      )
        throw new TransactionalHarnessError("source receipt wire unavailable");
      validateReceiptWire(response, "GET");
      if (!validator) throw new TransactionalHarnessError("source receipt validator unavailable");
      if (validator.validate("#/$defs/mutationReceipt", value).length)
        throw new TransactionalReceiptFailure("source receipt schema invalid");
      const body = value as Record<string, unknown>;
      if (body.status !== "completed" && body.status !== "failed")
        throw new TransactionalHarnessError("source receipt disposition unavailable");
      if (body.detail !== "available")
        throw new TransactionalHarnessError("source receipt exceeds inline fixture");
      let receipt: string;
      try {
        receipt = receiptUrl(body.id, scope);
      } catch {
        throw new TransactionalReceiptFailure("source receipt identity invalid");
      }
      if (!isDeepStrictEqual(body, JSON.parse(response.bodyText)))
        throw new TransactionalHarnessError("source receipt body disagrees with wire");
      let page: string | undefined;
      if (body.status === "completed" && body.next !== null) {
        try {
          page = receiptPageUrl(body.next, receipt);
        } catch {
          throw new TransactionalReceiptFailure("source receipt page identity invalid");
        }
      }
      if (pointer === "/next" && !page)
        throw new TransactionalReceiptFailure("source receipt page unavailable");
      if (ledger.has(binding) || ledger.size >= maximum)
        throw new TransactionalHarnessError("receipt authority binding limit or reuse");
      ledger.set(binding, {
        reference: Object.freeze({
          action,
          ...(pointer === "/next" ? { pointer, page: page! } : { pointer }),
          receipt,
          responseDigest: createHash("sha256").update(response.wireResponseBytes).digest("hex"),
          credentialRef: credential ?? config.defaultCredentialRef,
          session: Object.freeze({ ...served }),
        }),
        body: JSON.parse(response.bodyText),
        validator,
      });
    },
    resolve(binding: unknown, value: unknown) {
      const entry = typeof binding === "string" ? ledger.get(binding) : undefined;
      if (!entry)
        throw new TransactionalHarnessError("receipt not authorized by original wire observation");
      const source = entry.reference;
      const target = source.pointer === "/next" ? source.page : source.receipt;
      if (
        target !== value ||
        (source.pointer === "/next"
          ? receiptPageUrl(value, source.receipt)
          : receiptUrl(value, scope)) !== target
      )
        throw new TransactionalHarnessError("receipt not authorized by original wire observation");
      // The digest records original wire provenance. Later serving headers may
      // change, so agreement compares retained JSON facts rather than wire bytes.
      const active = session();
      if (
        active.binding.runtimeConfigurationIdentity !==
          source.session.runtimeConfigurationIdentity ||
        active.binding.configurationDigest !== source.session.configurationDigest
      )
        throw new TransactionalHarnessError("receipt session logical identity changed");
      return {
        source,
        target,
        active,
        validate(response: HttpExchangeResponse, method: "GET" | "HEAD") {
          validateReceiptWire(response, method);
          if (method === "GET") {
            let body: unknown;
            try {
              body = JSON.parse(response.bodyText);
            } catch {
              throw new TransactionalReceiptFailure("retained receipt JSON invalid");
            }
            if (source.pointer === "/next") {
              const page = body as Record<string, unknown> | null;
              if (
                entry.validator.validate("#/$defs/mutationReceiptPage", body).length ||
                page?.receipt !== source.receipt
              )
                throw new TransactionalReceiptFailure(
                  "retained receipt page schema or identity mismatch",
                );
              // A HEAD never initializes this baseline. An invalid first GET does not either.
              if (entry.pageBody === undefined) {
                if (page.next !== null)
                  throw new TransactionalHarnessError("receipt page exceeds single-page fixture");
                entry.pageBody = body;
              } else if (!isDeepStrictEqual(body, entry.pageBody))
                throw new TransactionalReceiptFailure(
                  "retained receipt page differs from original wire facts",
                );
              return;
            }
            if (!isDeepStrictEqual(body, entry.body))
              throw new TransactionalReceiptFailure(
                "retained receipt differs from original wire facts",
              );
          }
        },
      };
    },
  };
}

/** This bounded fixture requires the receiver's actual Content-Length encoding.
 * No general alternate transfer coding claim is made by this witness. */
export function validateReceiptWire(response: HttpExchangeResponse, method: "GET" | "HEAD") {
  const bytes = response.wireResponseBytes;
  if (!(bytes instanceof Uint8Array) || bytes.byteLength > 2097152)
    throw new TransactionalHarnessError("receipt wire evidence unavailable");
  const wire = Buffer.from(bytes),
    delimiter = wire.indexOf("\r\n\r\n");
  if (delimiter < 0 || delimiter > 65536)
    throw new TransactionalHarnessError("receipt wire header bound");
  const lines = wire.subarray(0, delimiter).toString("latin1").split("\r\n");
  if (lines.shift() !== `HTTP/1.1 ${response.status} OK`)
    throw new TransactionalHarnessError("receipt wire status mismatch");
  const actual: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const line of lines) {
    const colon = line.indexOf(":");
    if (colon < 1) throw new TransactionalHarnessError("receipt wire header mismatch");
    const name = line.slice(0, colon).toLowerCase(),
      value = line.slice(colon + 1).trim();
    if (Object.hasOwn(actual, name))
      throw new TransactionalHarnessError("duplicate receipt header");
    actual[name] = value;
  }
  const expected = Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(response.headers).map(([k, v]) => [k.toLowerCase(), v]),
  );
  if (!isDeepStrictEqual(actual, expected))
    throw new TransactionalHarnessError("receipt wire headers mismatch");
  const body = wire.subarray(delimiter + 4);
  if (
    body.length > 1048576 ||
    response.bodyOctets !== body.length ||
    !body.equals(Buffer.from(response.bodyText)) ||
    (method === "HEAD" && body.length !== 0) ||
    (method === "GET" && Number(actual["content-length"]) !== body.length)
  )
    throw new TransactionalHarnessError("receipt wire body mismatch");
}


===== packages/conformance/src/raw-http-scenario-target.ts whole-file SHA256 d1afc4d70a066a674db40caa5c26e846d7b6f78c98743b8a504eb4b38d73a581 complete file =====
import type { ConformanceFixture } from "./artifact-bundle.js";
import type { ExecutableScenario } from "./executable-manifest.js";
import {
  createRawHttpExchangeExecutor,
  type RawHttpExchangeExecutor,
  type ExactHttpExchangeRequest,
  type ExactHttpModeOptions,
  HttpTransportError,
  snapshotExactHttpMode,
  validateExactRunConfiguration,
  type HttpExchangeRequest,
  type RawHttpDialRoute,
  type RawHttpExchangeExecutorOptions,
} from "./http-executor.js";
import type { FixturePreparation, ScenarioHarness } from "./runner.js";

export interface RawHttpScenarioSession extends FixturePreparation {
  readonly dialRoute: RawHttpDialRoute;
  close(signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
}

export type StartRawHttpScenarioSession = (
  scenario: ExecutableScenario,
  scope: string,
  seed: number,
  fixture: ConformanceFixture,
  signal: AbortSignal,
) => Promise<RawHttpScenarioSession>;

export type RawHttpScenarioTargetOptions = RawHttpExchangeExecutorOptions & {
  /** Session cleanup lifecycle bound; defaults to requestTimeoutMs, then 30s. */
  readonly cleanupTimeoutMs?: number;
};

export type ExactHttpConfiguration = Omit<ExactHttpModeOptions, "resolveCredentials">;

export interface RawHttpScenarioTarget {
  /** Actual frozen harness configuration, without the credential resolver or credential bytes. */
  readonly exactConfiguration?: ExactHttpConfiguration;
  readonly execute: RawHttpExchangeExecutor;
  /** Fetch-compatible view of the active public target for programmable client diagnostics. */
  readonly fetch: typeof fetch;
  readonly harness: ScenarioHarness;
  /** Emergency cleanup for a run that exits outside the runner's normal lifecycle. */
  close(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
}

/**
 * Composes a sequential scenario harness with the raw HTTP executor without
 * letting target identity, fixture preparation, or the socket dial route leak
 * into the semantic request URL.
 */
export function createRawHttpScenarioTarget(
  start: StartRawHttpScenarioSession,
  options: RawHttpScenarioTargetOptions = {},
): RawHttpScenarioTarget {
  if (typeof start !== "function") throw new TypeError("start must be a function");
  const snapshot = Object.freeze({
    ...options,
    ...(options.exactMode === undefined
      ? {}
      : { exactMode: snapshotExactHttpMode(options.exactMode) }),
  });
  if (
    snapshot.requestTimeoutMs !== undefined &&
    (!Number.isSafeInteger(snapshot.requestTimeoutMs) ||
      snapshot.requestTimeoutMs <= 0 ||
      snapshot.requestTimeoutMs > 2_147_483_647)
  )
    throw new RangeError("requestTimeoutMs must be a positive timer bound");
  const cleanupTimeoutMs = snapshot.cleanupTimeoutMs ?? snapshot.requestTimeoutMs ?? 30_000;
  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(cleanupTimeoutMs) ||
    cleanupTimeoutMs <= 0 ||
    cleanupTimeoutMs > 2_147_483_647
  )
    throw new RangeError(
      `${snapshot.cleanupTimeoutMs === undefined ? "requestTimeoutMs" : "cleanupTimeoutMs"} must be a positive timer bound`,
    );
  let preparation:
    | { controller: AbortController; settled: Promise<void>; finish: () => void }
    | undefined;
  let closing: Promise<void> | undefined;
  let active:
    | {
        readonly execute: RawHttpExchangeExecutor;
        readonly close: RawHttpScenarioSession["close"];
        readonly controller: AbortController;
        readonly pending: Set<Promise<unknown>>;
      }
    | undefined;

  const close = (signal: AbortSignal = new AbortController().signal): Promise<void> => {
    if (closing !== undefined) return closing;
    const ending = active;
    active = undefined;
    const starting = preparation;
    starting?.controller.abort();
    if (ending === undefined && starting === undefined) return Promise.resolve();
    ending?.controller.abort();
    const cleanupSignal = AbortSignal.any([signal, AbortSignal.timeout(cleanupTimeoutMs)]);
    const cleanup = Promise.resolve().then(async () => {
      if (starting !== undefined) await starting.settled;
      if (ending !== undefined) {
        await Promise.allSettled([...ending.pending]);
        await ending.close(cleanupSignal);
      }
    });
    closing = settleCleanup(cleanup, cleanupSignal).finally(() => {
      closing = undefined;
    });
    return closing;
  };

  const harness: ScenarioHarness = {
    prepare: async (scenario, scope, seed, fixture, signal) => {
      if (active !== undefined || preparation !== undefined || closing !== undefined)
        throw new Error("raw HTTP scenario target already has an active fixture");
      if (snapshot.exactMode !== undefined && snapshot.exactMode.scope !== scope)
        throw new HttpTransportError(
          "configuration",
          "prepared Scope does not match configured exact mode",
        );
      const controller = new AbortController();
      let finish = (): void => undefined;
      const settled = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
        finish = resolve;
      });
      const starting = { controller, settled, finish };
      preparation = starting;
      const preparingSignal = AbortSignal.any([signal, controller.signal]);
      try {
        const session = await start(scenario, scope, seed, fixture, preparingSignal);
        const closeStarted = (): Promise<void> => {
          const closeSignal = AbortSignal.timeout(cleanupTimeoutMs);
          return settleCleanup(
            Promise.resolve().then(() => session.close(closeSignal)),
            closeSignal,
          );
        };
        if (preparingSignal.aborted) {
          await closeStarted();
          throw new Error("raw HTTP scenario target preparation was aborted");
        }
        try {
          const execute = createRawHttpExchangeExecutor(session.dialRoute, snapshot);
          const prepared = {
            capabilities: Object.freeze([...session.capabilities]),
            ...(session.bindings === undefined
              ? {}
              : { bindings: Object.freeze({ ...session.bindings }) }),
          };
          active = {
            execute,
            close: (closeSignal) => session.close(closeSignal),
            controller: new AbortController(),
            pending: new Set(),
          };
          return prepared;
        } catch (error) {
          await closeStarted();
          throw error;
        }
      } finally {
        if (preparation === starting) preparation = undefined;
        starting.finish();
      }
    },
    cleanup: async (_scenario, _scope, signal) => close(signal),
  };

  const mode = snapshot.exactMode;
  const exactConfiguration: ExactHttpConfiguration | undefined =
    mode === undefined
      ? undefined
      : Object.freeze({
          scope: mode.scope,
          profile: mode.profile,
          routes: mode.routes,
          maximumRequestHeaderBytes: mode.maximumRequestHeaderBytes,
          maximumRequestBodyBytes: mode.maximumRequestBodyBytes,
          defaultCredentialRef: mode.defaultCredentialRef,
          credentialHandles: mode.credentialHandles,
        });
  return Object.freeze({
    ...(exactConfiguration === undefined ? {} : { exactConfiguration }),
    execute: dispatch,
    fetch: async (input: string | URL | Request, init?: RequestInit) => {
      const request = new Request(input, init);
      if (request.body !== null)
        throw new TypeError("raw HTTP scenario Fetch does not support request bodies");
      if (!isSupportedMethod(request.method))
        throw new TypeError("raw HTTP scenario Fetch received an unsupported method");
      const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
      request.headers.forEach((value, name) => {
        headers[name] = value;
      });
      const response = await dispatch({
        method: request.method,
        url: request.url,
        headers,
        signal: request.signal,
      });
      const fetchResponse = new Response(
        response.bodyText.length === 0 ? null : response.bodyText,
        {
          status: response.status,
          headers: response.headers,
        },
      );
      Object.defineProperty(fetchResponse, "url", { value: response.url });
      return fetchResponse;
    },
    harness,
    close,
  });

  function dispatch(
    request: HttpExchangeRequest | ExactHttpExchangeRequest,
  ): ReturnType<RawHttpExchangeExecutor> {
    const session = active;
    if (session === undefined)
      return Promise.reject(
        new Error("raw HTTP scenario target executed outside a prepared fixture"),
      );
    let inspected = false;
    let exactBoundary = snapshot.exactMode !== undefined;
    try {
      const exactArm = "raw" in request;
      inspected = true;
      exactBoundary ||= exactArm;
      if (exactBoundary) {
        const prototype = Object.getPrototypeOf(request);
        if (prototype !== null && prototype !== Object.prototype)
          return Promise.reject(
            new HttpTransportError(
              "configuration",
              "exact HTTP session request was refused",
              {},
              "not-started",
            ),
          );
      }
      // The executor snapshots exact data descriptors; avoid invoking accessors through object spread.
      const descriptors = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(request);
      const signalDescriptor = descriptors.signal;
      if (
        signalDescriptor === undefined ||
        !("value" in signalDescriptor) ||
        !(signalDescriptor.value instanceof AbortSignal)
      )
        throw new HttpTransportError("configuration", "HTTP request signal is invalid");

      if (snapshot.exactMode !== undefined) {
        if (
          !descriptors.url ||
          !("value" in descriptors.url) ||
          !descriptors.method ||
          !("value" in descriptors.method)
        )
          throw new HttpTransportError("configuration", "HTTP target is invalid");
        validateExactRunConfiguration(
          snapshot.exactMode,
          descriptors.url.value,
          descriptors.method.value,
        );
      }
      const captured = Object.defineProperties(Object.create(null), {
        ...descriptors,
        signal: {
          value: AbortSignal.any([signalDescriptor.value, session.controller.signal]),
          enumerable: true,
        },
      });
      const operation = session.execute(captured);
      session.pending.add(operation);
      void operation.then(
        () => session.pending.delete(operation),
        () => session.pending.delete(operation),
      );
      return operation;
    } catch (error) {
      if (exactBoundary || !inspected)
        return Promise.reject(
          new HttpTransportError(
            "configuration",
            "exact HTTP session request was refused",
            {},
            "not-started",
          ),
        );
      return Promise.reject(error);
    }
  }
}

function isSupportedMethod(method: string): method is HttpExchangeRequest["method"] {
  return ["GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS", "POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE"].includes(method);
}

async function settleCleanup(operation: Promise<void>, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
  let abort: (() => void) | undefined;
  const aborted = new Promise<never>((_resolve, reject) => {
    abort = () => reject(new HttpTransportError("abort", "raw HTTP session cleanup was aborted"));
    if (signal.aborted) abort();
    else signal.addEventListener("abort", abort, { once: true });
  });
  try {
    await Promise.race([operation, aborted]);
  } finally {
    if (abort !== undefined) signal.removeEventListener("abort", abort);
  }
}


===== apps/bdptest/test-support/transactional-execution-binding.ts whole-file SHA256 cde506c72a8710eafe58522900f6db70ed3701cf333560252dc8e7c5e706cf89 complete file =====
import { isDeepStrictEqual } from "node:util";
import { installedContractsDigest } from "./installed-contract-identity.js";
/** Source-only installed binding. Runner retains sole receipt observation authority. */
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import {
  createRawHttpScenarioTarget,
  type ExactHttpModeOptions,
  type ConformanceFixture,
  type ExecutableScenario,
} from "@bdp/conformance";
import {
  bindServedProfileConfiguration,
  assertSameServedProfileConfiguration,
  type TransactionalRunContext,
  type TransactionalSession,
} from "@bdp/conformance/testing";
export interface InstalledTransactionChild {
  readonly ready: Record<string, unknown>;
  close(): Promise<unknown>;
}
export function installedTransactionBinding(
  ready: Record<string, unknown>,
  sourceCommit: string,
  fixture: { bytes: Uint8Array },
) {
  if (
    ready.profile !== "transactional" ||
    ready.history !== false ||
    ready.claimEligible !== false ||
    ready.authorityMode !== "canonical" ||
    typeof ready.scope !== "string" ||
    typeof ready.runtimeConfigurationIdentity !== "string" ||
    !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(ready.runtimeConfigurationIdentity)
  )
    throw new Error("installed Transactional metadata unavailable");
  if (typeof ready.listenUrl !== "string")
    throw new Error("installed physical session unavailable");
  const url = new URL(ready.listenUrl);
  if (
    url.protocol !== "http:" ||
    url.hostname !== "127.0.0.1" ||
    !url.port ||
    url.username ||
    url.password ||
    url.pathname !== "/" ||
    url.search ||
    url.hash
  )
    throw new Error("invalid installed dial binding");
  const targetNames = {
    createBead: "create-bead",
    updateBead: "update-bead",
    deleteBead: "delete-bead",
    createLink: "create-link",
    updateLink: "update-link",
    deleteLink: "delete-link",
    putAlias: "put-alias",
    deleteAlias: "delete-alias",
    updateWhere: "update-where",
    deleteWhere: "delete-where",
    batch: "batch",
    sequence: "sequence",
  };
  const operationTargets = Object.freeze(
    Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(targetNames).map(([name, suffix]) => [
        name,
        `${ready.scope}operations/${suffix}`,
      ]),
    ),
  );
  if (!isDeepStrictEqual(ready.targets, operationTargets))
    throw new Error("installed operation target inventory mismatch");
  const served = bindServedProfileConfiguration({
    scope: ready.scope,
    profile: "transactional",
    history: false,
    sourceCommit,
    installedPayloadDigest: ready.payloadIdentity as string,
    installedContractsDigest: installedContractsDigest(ready.artifactIdentity),
    policyDigest: ready.policyIdentity as string,
    authenticationDigest: ready.authenticationIdentity as string,
    fixture,
  });
  return Object.freeze({
    served,
    operationTargets,
    runtimeConfigurationIdentity: ready.runtimeConfigurationIdentity,
    authorityMode: "canonical" as const,
  });
}
export function createTransactionalExecutionBinding(options: {
  readonly baseline: ReturnType<typeof installedTransactionBinding>;
  readonly sourceCommit: string;
  readonly fixtureSource: { bytes: Uint8Array };
  readonly exactMode: ExactHttpModeOptions;
  readonly launch: (
    scenario: string,
    reopen: boolean,
    signal: AbortSignal,
  ) => Promise<InstalledTransactionChild>;
}) {
  let active:
    | (TransactionalSession & { controller: AbortController; pending: Set<Promise<unknown>> })
    | undefined;
  let generation = 0;
  let presentation:
    | { scenario: ExecutableScenario; scope: string; seed: number; fixture: ConformanceFixture }
    | undefined;
  let reopening = false;
  const target = createRawHttpScenarioTarget(
    async (scenario, scope, seed, fixture, signal) => {
      const child = await options.launch(scenario.id, reopening, signal);
      try {
        if (signal.aborted) throw new Error("installed preparation aborted");
        const actual = installedTransactionBinding(
          child.ready,
          options.sourceCommit,
          options.fixtureSource,
        );
        assertSameServedProfileConfiguration(options.baseline.served, actual.served);
        if (
          actual.runtimeConfigurationIdentity !== options.baseline.runtimeConfigurationIdentity ||
          actual.authorityMode !== options.baseline.authorityMode
        )
          throw new Error("installed runtime identity mismatch");
        const listenUrl = child.ready.listenUrl as string,
          address = new URL(listenUrl);
        const controller = new AbortController(),
          pending = new Set<Promise<unknown>>();
        const binding = Object.freeze({
          scenario: scenario.id,
          generation: ++generation,
          listenUrl,
          runtimeConfigurationIdentity: actual.runtimeConfigurationIdentity,
          responseLimits: Object.freeze({
            headerBytes: 65536 as const,
            bodyBytes: 1048576 as const,
            wireBytes: 2097152 as const,
          }),
          configurationDigest: createHash("sha256")
            .update(JSON.stringify(actual.served))
            .digest("hex"),
        });
        const current = {
          binding,
          controller,
          pending,
          signal: AbortSignal.any([controller.signal, signal]),
          dialRoute: Object.freeze({
            transport: "plain" as const,
            host: address.hostname,
            port: Number(address.port),
          }),
          resolveCredentials: options.exactMode.resolveCredentials,
          register<T>(operation: Promise<T>) {
            pending.add(operation);
            void operation.then(
              () => pending.delete(operation),
              () => pending.delete(operation),
            );
            return operation;
          },
        };
        active = current;
        presentation = { scenario, scope, seed, fixture };
        return {
          capabilities: fixture.capabilities,
          bindings: fixture.bindings,
          dialRoute: current.dialRoute,
          async close() {
            if (active === current) active = undefined;
            controller.abort();
            await Promise.allSettled([...pending]);
            await child.close();
          },
        };
      } catch (error) {
        try {
          await child.close();
        } catch (cleanup) {
          throw new AggregateError([error, cleanup], "installed session setup cleanup failed");
        }
        throw error;
      }
    },
    {
      exactMode: options.exactMode,
      requestTimeoutMs: 5000,
      cleanupTimeoutMs: 10000,
      maximumHeaderBytes: 65536,
      maximumBodyBytes: 1048576,
    },
  );
  const context: TransactionalRunContext = {
    current(scenario) {
      if (!active || active.binding.scenario !== scenario || active.signal.aborted)
        throw new Error("installed session unavailable");
      return active;
    },
    async reopen(scenario, signal) {
      const old = presentation;
      if (!old || old.scenario.id !== scenario || !active)
        throw new Error("reopen outside active scenario");
      await target.close(signal);
      reopening = true;
      try {
        await target.harness.prepare(old.scenario, old.scope, old.seed, old.fixture, signal);
      } finally {
        reopening = false;
      }
    },
  };
  return Object.freeze({ target, context });
}


===== packages/server/src/transaction-sequence.ts whole-file SHA256 019e02f2f392047d568d7464b1c5af0482d6d743ed09a6d0aa4edab151607cbe complete file =====
/** Private bounded Transactional sequence carrier. No HTTP or profile admission. */
import { setImmediate } from "node:timers/promises";
import { parseReadUpdateProblem } from "@bdp/protocol";
import type { PreparedTransactionSequence } from "./transaction-batch.js";
import type { openTransactionStore, TransactionSequenceTurn } from "./transaction-store.js";

type Entry = Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>;
export interface TransactionSequenceResponse {
  readonly results: readonly Entry[];
}
const problems = {
  "idempotency-conflict": ["conflict", 409, "never"],
  "idempotency-in-progress": ["conflict", 409, "after-delay"],
  "idempotency-expired": ["gone", 410, "never"],
} as const;
function problem(code: keyof typeof problems): Entry {
  const [family, status, retry] = problems[code];
  return {
    ...parseReadUpdateProblem({
      type: `https://github.com/gastownhall/bdp/problems/${family}`,
      code,
      status,
      retry,
    }),
  };
}
function record(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value))
    throw new Error("invalid retained sequence disposition");
  return value as Record<string, unknown>;
}
function project(sequence: PreparedTransactionSequence, turn: TransactionSequenceTurn): Entry {
  const member = sequence.members[turn.index];
  if (!member) throw new Error("sequence turn outside carrier");
  let entry: Record<string, unknown>;
  if (turn.result.kind === "transient") entry = { ...problem("idempotency-in-progress") };
  else if (turn.result.kind === "conflict") entry = { ...problem("idempotency-conflict") };
  else {
    const receipt = turn.result.receipt;
    if (receipt.status === "pending") entry = { ...problem("idempotency-in-progress") };
    else if (receipt.detail === "expired") {
      if (receipt.status !== "completed") throw new Error("expired noncompleted receipt");
      entry = { ...problem("idempotency-expired") };
      if (member.operation === "createBead" || member.operation === "createLink") {
        if (!Array.isArray(receipt.allocated) || receipt.allocated.length !== 1)
          throw new Error("expired singleton creation lost its allocation");
        const allocated = record(receipt.allocated[0]);
        if (
          allocated.operationIndex !== 0 ||
          typeof allocated.id !== "string" ||
          typeof allocated.type !== "string"
        )
          throw new Error("invalid expired singleton allocation");
        entry.allocated = { id: allocated.id, type: allocated.type };
      }
    } else if (receipt.detail !== "available")
      throw new Error("unsupported private receipt disclosure");
    else if (receipt.status === "failed") entry = { ...record(receipt.problem) };
    else {
      if (!Array.isArray(receipt.results) || receipt.results.length !== 1 || receipt.next !== null)
        throw new Error("singleton sequence receipt must have exactly one result");
      entry = { ...record(receipt.results[0]) };
    }
  }
  // The current presentation supplies the position and label. Retained result
  // labels belong to the original carrier and are not semantic identity.
  delete entry.operationIndex;
  delete entry.operationName;
  return Object.freeze({
    ...entry,
    operationIndex: turn.index,
    ...(member.name === undefined ? {} : { operationName: member.name }),
  });
}
/** Source-private stepping gives unrelated owner calls a turn between commits.
 * Cancellation models an authority abort, not client disconnection. */
export function createTransactionSequenceStepper(
  store: ReturnType<typeof openTransactionStore>,
  principal: string,
  sequence: PreparedTransactionSequence,
) {
  const admission = store.admitSequence(principal, sequence);
  const results: Entry[] = [];
  let done = false,
    cancelled = false;
  return Object.freeze({
    step(): {
      readonly done: boolean;
      readonly entry: Entry;
      readonly response?: TransactionSequenceResponse;
    } {
      if (done || cancelled) throw new Error("sequence already complete or cancelled");
      try {
        const turn = store.nextSequence(admission);
        const entry = project(sequence, turn);
        results.push(entry);
        done = turn.done;
        return Object.freeze({
          done,
          entry,
          ...(done ? { response: Object.freeze({ results: Object.freeze([...results]) }) } : {}),
        });
      } catch (error) {
        cancelled = true;
        try {
          store.cancelSequence(admission);
        } catch (cleanup) {
          throw new AggregateError([error, cleanup], "sequence projection and cleanup failed");
        }
        throw error;
      }
    },
    abort(): number {
      const count = store.cancelSequence(admission);
      cancelled = true;
      return count;
    },
  });
}
/** Once admitted, response consumption does not control execution. Each turn
 * yields to unrelated calls; an internal failure retracts only this attempt's
 * remaining reservations and preserves its already committed prefix. */
export async function runTransactionSequence(
  store: ReturnType<typeof openTransactionStore>,
  principal: string,
  sequence: PreparedTransactionSequence,
): Promise<TransactionSequenceResponse> {
  const stepper = createTransactionSequenceStepper(store, principal, sequence);
  for (;;) {
    await setImmediate();
    const step = stepper.step();
    if (step.done) {
      if (!step.response) throw new Error("completed sequence lost response");
      return step.response;
    }
  }
}


===== packages/server/src/transaction-events.ts whole-file SHA256 c6052433662fcb2d0a202ad4e408a24d4c43f980313fa89659501b4475790db5 complete file =====
/** Private finite projection of committed facts. No pruning, mutable views,
 * SSE, replication bootstrap or public profile admission. */
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { TransactionMinimumFailure } from "./transaction-consistency.js";
import {
  parseCanonicalScope,
  resolveCanonicalLocalResourceId,
  stringifyJsonValue,
  type Reference,
} from "@bdp/protocol";
import {
  TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS,
  type TransactionEvent,
  type TransactionStateChange,
} from "./transaction-batch.js";
import type { TransactionReadContext } from "./transaction-store.js";

export const TRANSACTION_EVENT_PAGE_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024;
/** Actual private stream mechanics, not public paging limits. */
export const TRANSACTION_EVENT_STREAM = Object.freeze({
  maximumGroupsPerObservation: 1,
  packetBytes: TRANSACTION_EVENT_PAGE_BYTES,
});
// Same closed-shape proof as the admitted tuple in the implementation progress
// document. This bounds encoded group bytes, not parsed object/heap overhead.
const R = TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.representationBytes,
  Q = TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.requestBodyBytes,
  S = TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.scopeSourceBytes,
  N = 7 * Q,
  A = 8192,
  H = 1048576,
  K = TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.matchedResources + TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.maximumOperations;
export const TRANSACTION_EVENT_GROUP_BYTES = Math.min(
  TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.inducedEvents * (2.5 * R + N + S) + K * A + H,
  K * (Math.max(9 * R + 4 * S, 5 * R + 2 * N + 2 * S) + A) + H,
);
// Largest owned update <= 2R+N+source+A. A Resource source is at most R
// plus its view suffix; the page repeats it in source and next. Reserve 1024
// extra bytes for suffixes, cursor and envelope. Scope source is smaller.
export const TRANSACTION_EVENT_FIRST_PAGE_BYTES = 5 * R + N + A + 1024;
if (TRANSACTION_EVENT_FIRST_PAGE_BYTES > TRANSACTION_EVENT_PAGE_BYTES)
  throw new Error("transaction tuple cannot support the finite Event page budget");
export class TransactionEventProjectionGap extends Error {}
export class TransactionEventParameterError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message?: string,
    readonly context?: TransactionReadContext,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}
export interface TransactionEventSource {
  readonly url: string;
  readonly subject?: string;
  readonly kind: "scope" | "bead" | "link";
}
export interface TransactionEventGroup {
  readonly scopeEpoch: string;
  readonly authorizationView: string;
  readonly position: string;
  readonly checkpoint: string;
  readonly transaction: string;
  readonly eventCount: number;
  readonly events: readonly TransactionEvent[];
  readonly changes: readonly TransactionStateChange[];
}
export interface TransactionEventPage {
  readonly source: string;
  readonly events: readonly TransactionEvent[];
  readonly next: string | null;
}
const ordinal = /^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$/;
const position = /^pos-(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$/;
const cursor =
  /^ev_([A-Za-z0-9_-]{43})_([A-Za-z0-9_-]{43})_([A-Za-z0-9_-]{43})_(pos-(?:0|[1-9][0-9]*))_(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$/;
const hash = (tag: string, value: string) =>
  createHash("sha256")
    .update(JSON.stringify([tag, value]))
    .digest("base64url");

/** Scope and Resource token spelling is deliberately private, not a fixture law. */
export function renderTransactionEvent(
  context: Pick<TransactionReadContext, "scopeEpoch" | "authorizationView">,
  source: string,
  checkpoint: string,
  event: TransactionEvent,
): TransactionEvent {
  if (
    !position.test(checkpoint) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(Number(checkpoint.slice(4))) ||
    Number(checkpoint.slice(4)) < 1 ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(event.ordinal) ||
    event.ordinal < 0
  )
    throw new Error("invalid committed Event position");
  return {
    ...event,
    source,
    id: `ev_${hash("epoch", context.scopeEpoch)}_${hash("view", context.authorizationView)}_${hash("source", source)}_${checkpoint}_${event.ordinal}`,
  };
}
export function parseTransactionEventCursor(
  value: string,
  context?: TransactionReadContext,
): {
  position: number;
  ordinal: number;
  epoch: string;
  view: string;
  source: string;
} {
  const match = value.length <= 256 ? cursor.exec(value) : null;
  if (!match || !ordinal.test(match[5] ?? ""))
    throw new TransactionEventParameterError(undefined, context);
  const p = Number(match[4]?.slice(4)),
    o = Number(match[5]);
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(p) || p < 1 || !Number.isSafeInteger(o) || o < 0)
    throw new TransactionEventParameterError(undefined, context);
  return {
    position: p,
    ordinal: o,
    epoch: match[1] as string,
    view: match[2] as string,
    source: match[3] as string,
  };
}
export function checkTransactionEventCursor(
  value: ReturnType<typeof parseTransactionEventCursor>,
  context: TransactionReadContext,
  source: string,
): void {
  // Opaque context tags diagnose incompatible context; they are not credentials
  // or proof of prior issuance. Matching context still requires source membership.
  if (value.epoch !== hash("epoch", context.scopeEpoch))
    throw new TransactionMinimumFailure("cursor-expired", context);
  if (value.view !== hash("view", context.authorizationView))
    throw new TransactionMinimumFailure("foreign-view", context);
  if (value.source !== hash("source", source))
    throw new TransactionEventParameterError(undefined, context);
}

/** Undefined means this is not an Event route; malformed Event requests throw. */
export function transactionEventRequest(
  url: URL,
  scopeText: string,
): { readonly source: TransactionEventSource; readonly after?: string } | undefined {
  const scope = parseCanonicalScope(scopeText),
    base = new URL(scope);
  const scopeSource = new URL("events/", scope).href;
  const wantsResource = url.searchParams.getAll("view").includes("events");
  if (url.origin !== base.origin || !url.pathname.startsWith(base.pathname)) return undefined;
  const scopeRoute = url.origin + url.pathname === scopeSource;
  if (!scopeRoute && !wantsResource) return undefined;
  const allowed = scopeRoute ? ["after"] : ["view", "after"];
  for (const key of url.searchParams.keys())
    if (!allowed.includes(key) || url.searchParams.getAll(key).length !== 1)
      throw new TransactionEventParameterError();
  if (url.hash) throw new TransactionEventParameterError();
  let source: TransactionEventSource;
  if (scopeRoute) source = { kind: "scope", url: scopeSource };
  else {
    const local = url.pathname.slice(base.pathname.length),
      kind = local.startsWith("beads/") ? "bead" : local.startsWith("links/") ? "link" : undefined;
    if (!kind || url.searchParams.get("view") !== "events")
      throw new TransactionEventParameterError();
    try {
      const subject = resolveCanonicalLocalResourceId(scope, kind, local);
      if (new URL(subject).pathname !== url.pathname) throw new Error("noncanonical Event subject");
      source = { kind, subject, url: `${subject}?view=events` };
    } catch {
      throw new TransactionEventParameterError();
    }
  }
  const after = url.searchParams.get("after");
  if (after !== null) parseTransactionEventCursor(after);
  return Object.freeze({ source: Object.freeze(source), ...(after === null ? {} : { after }) });
}
function reference(value: unknown): string {
  if (typeof value === "string") return value;
  if (
    value &&
    typeof value === "object" &&
    typeof (value as Reference & { uri?: unknown }).uri === "string"
  )
    return (value as { uri: string }).uri;
  throw new Error("invalid retained Event endpoint");
}

/** Uses only this group's final upserts/creation/incidence facts, never live Links. */
export function projectTransactionEventGroup(
  value: unknown,
  context: TransactionReadContext,
  source: TransactionEventSource,
  scope: string,
  expectedPosition: number,
): readonly TransactionEvent[] {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object") throw new Error("invalid retained Event group");
  const group = value as TransactionEventGroup;
  if (
    group.scopeEpoch !== context.scopeEpoch ||
    group.authorizationView !== context.authorizationView ||
    group.position !== `pos-${expectedPosition}` ||
    group.checkpoint !== group.position ||
    !Array.isArray(group.events) ||
    group.eventCount !== group.events.length ||
    !Array.isArray(group.changes) ||
    typeof group.transaction !== "string"
  )
    throw new Error("retained Event group context mismatch");
  const endpoints = new Map<string, readonly [unknown, unknown]>();
  const linkIds = new Set<string>();
  const remember = (id: string, from: unknown, to: unknown) => {
    reference(from);
    reference(to);
    const old = endpoints.get(id),
      next = [from, to] as const;
    if (old && stringifyJsonValue(old) !== stringifyJsonValue(next))
      throw new Error("inconsistent retained Link endpoints");
    endpoints.set(id, next);
  };
  for (const change of group.changes) {
    if (change.resourceKind === "link") {
      linkIds.add(change.resource.id);
      if (change.operation === "upsert" && "source" in change.resource)
        remember(change.resource.id, change.resource.source, change.resource.target);
    }
  }
  for (const event of group.events) {
    const data = event.data as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (!data || typeof data !== "object") throw new Error("invalid retained Event data");
    if (
      event.type === "linked" ||
      event.type === "unlinked" ||
      (event.type === "created" && linkIds.has(event.subject))
    )
      remember(event.subject, data.source, data.target);
  }
  const result: TransactionEvent[] = [];
  for (const [index, event] of group.events.entries()) {
    if (
      event.ordinal !== index ||
      event.source !== `${scope}events/` ||
      event.transaction !== group.transaction ||
      !["created", "updated", "deleted", "linked", "unlinked"].includes(event.type)
    )
      throw new Error("invalid retained Event framing");
    const data = event.data as Record<string, unknown>;
    let include = source.kind === "scope";
    if (source.kind !== "scope") {
      include =
        event.subject === source.subject && ["created", "updated", "deleted"].includes(event.type);
      if (source.kind === "bead" && (event.type === "linked" || event.type === "unlinked")) {
        if (data.endpoint !== "source" && data.endpoint !== "target")
          throw new Error("invalid endpoint fact");
        include = reference(data[data.endpoint]) === source.subject;
      }
      if (source.kind === "bead" && event.type === "updated" && linkIds.has(event.subject)) {
        const pair = endpoints.get(event.subject);
        if (!pair) throw new Error("incident Link update lacks same-group endpoints");
        include = pair.some((endpoint) => reference(endpoint) === source.subject);
        // Literal incident rule currently mentions properties only. Do not silently
        // decide metadata-only membership while the spec disposition is pending.
        if (include && data.change === undefined && data.metadataChange !== undefined)
          throw new TransactionEventProjectionGap("incident-link-metadata");
      }
    }
    if (include) result.push(renderTransactionEvent(context, source.url, group.checkpoint, event));
  }
  return result;
}
export function eventContinuation(source: string, id: string): string {
  const next = new URL(source);
  next.searchParams.set("after", id);
  return next.href;
}
export function freezeEventPage(page: TransactionEventPage): TransactionEventPage {
  const pending: unknown[] = [page];
  while (pending.length) {
    const value = pending.pop();
    if (value && typeof value === "object" && !Object.isFrozen(value)) {
      pending.push(...Object.values(value));
      Object.freeze(value);
    }
  }
  return page;
}

export async function runConformanceMatrix(
  options: ScenarioRunOptions,
): Promise<ConformanceRunResult> {
  return runMatrix(options, false);
}

/** Source-private installed observation; never public Transactional admission. */
export async function runCumulativeTransactionalMatrix(
  options: ScenarioRunOptions,
  context?: TransactionalRunContext,
): Promise<ConformanceRunResult> {
  return runMatrix(options, true, context);
}

async function runMatrix(
  options: ScenarioRunOptions,
  transactional: boolean,
  context?: TransactionalRunContext,
): Promise<ConformanceRunResult> {
  const runOptions = snapshotRunOptions(options);
  const branded = isCumulativeTransactionalArtifactBundle(runOptions.artifactBundle);
  if (transactional ? !branded || runOptions.profile !== "transactional" : branded)
    throw new ConformanceRunnerError(
      "private Transactional bundle requires its matching private runner and profile",
    );
  validateRunOptions(runOptions, transactional);
  const { catalog, manifest } = runOptions.artifactBundle;
  const metadata = selectApplicableScenariosForProfile(catalog, runOptions.profile);
  const allCatalogIds = new Set(catalog.scenarios.map(({ id }) => id));
  const unknownPlan = manifest.scenarios.find(({ id }) => !allCatalogIds.has(id));
  if (unknownPlan !== undefined)
    throw new ConformanceRunnerError(`executable plan '${unknownPlan.id}' has no catalog metadata`);
  const byId = new Map(manifest.scenarios.map((scenario) => [scenario.id, scenario]));
  let selected =
    runOptions.scenarioFilter === undefined
      ? metadata
      : metadata.filter(({ id }) => id.includes(runOptions.scenarioFilter as string));
  if (runOptions.scenarioFilter !== undefined && selected.length === 0)
    throw new ConformanceRunnerError("scenarioFilter matched no applicable scenarios");
  if (runOptions.scenarioSelection !== undefined) {
    const applicable = new Set(metadata.map(({ id }) => id));
    const missing = runOptions.scenarioSelection.filter((id) => !applicable.has(id));
    if (missing.length > 0)
      throw new ConformanceRunnerError(
        `scenarioSelection names scenarios that are unknown or inapplicable: ${missing.join(", ")}`,
      );
    const requested = new Set(runOptions.scenarioSelection);
    // Catalog order is preserved: the selection names membership, not sequence.
    selected = metadata.filter(({ id }) => requested.has(id));
  }
  preflightExactPlans(
    selected.flatMap(({ id }) => byId.get(id) ?? []),
    runOptions,
  );
  const results: ScenarioRunResult[] = [];
  const resultsById = new Map<string, ScenarioRunResult>();
  for (const [index, scenario] of selected.entries()) {
    if (runOptions.signal?.aborted === true) {
      for (const remaining of selected.slice(index)) results.push(stoppedScenarioResult(remaining));
      break;
    }
    const plan = byId.get(scenario.id);
    const scenarioResult =
      plan === undefined
        ? missingPlanResult(scenario)
        : (prerequisiteResultBeforeRun(scenario, plan, resultsById, catalog) ??
          (await runScenario(scenario, plan, runOptions, context)));
    results.push(scenarioResult);
    resultsById.set(scenarioResult.id, scenarioResult);
    if (mustStopMatrixAfter(scenarioResult)) {
      for (const remaining of selected.slice(results.length))
        results.push(stoppedScenarioResult(remaining));
      break;
    }
  }
  const common = {
    scope: runOptions.scope,
    profile: runOptions.profile,
    seed: runOptions.seed,
    selectedScenarioIds: selected.map(({ id }) => id),
    ...(runOptions.scenarioFilter === undefined
      ? {}
      : { scenarioFilter: runOptions.scenarioFilter }),
    ...(runOptions.scenarioSelection === undefined
      ? {}
      : { scenarioSelection: runOptions.scenarioSelection }),
    artifacts: runOptions.artifactBundle.digests,
    declarations: {
      targetLabel: runOptions.declaredTargetLabel,
      ...(runOptions.artifactBundle.manifest.manifestVersion === 2 &&
      runOptions.exactExecution !== undefined
        ? { exactConfigurationDigest: exactConfigurationDigest(runOptions.exactExecution) }
        : {}),
    },
    claimEligible: false as const,
  };
  if (runOptions.artifactBundle.manifest.manifestVersion === 2)
    return { ...common, reportVersion: 4, scenarios: results };
  const legacy = results.map((scenario) => {
    assertLegacyScenario(scenario);
    return scenario;
  });
  return { ...common, reportVersion: 3, scenarios: legacy };
}

export function assertLegacyScenario(
  scenario: ScenarioRunResult,
): asserts scenario is ScenarioRunResult & LegacyScenarioRunResult {
  for (const exchange of scenario.exchanges) {
    if (
      Object.hasOwn(exchange.request, "exact") ||
      Object.hasOwn(exchange, "harnessError") ||
      TRANSACTIONAL_EXCHANGE_FIELDS.some((key) => Object.hasOwn(exchange, key)) ||
      (exchange.transportError !== undefined &&
        Object.hasOwn(exchange.transportError, "writeState"))
    )
      throw new ConformanceRunnerError("exact observations cannot enter a legacy report");
  }
}

function mustStopMatrixAfter(result: ScenarioRunResult): boolean {
  const category = result.category;
  if (result.cleanupError !== undefined) return true;
  if (
    result.prerequisiteFailure?.phase === "recheck" &&
    result.prerequisiteFailure.category !== undefined
  )
    return true;
  switch (category) {
    case "aborted":
    case "cleanup":
    case "deadline":
    case "out-of-scope-target":
    case "exact-configuration":
    case "exact-observation":
      return true;
    case undefined:
    case "not-implemented":
    case "not-run":
    case "observation-limit":
    case "wire-observation-unavailable":
      return false;
    default: {
      const exhaustive: never = category;
      void exhaustive;
      return true;
    }
  }
}

function snapshotRunOptions(options: ScenarioRunOptions): ScenarioRunOptions {
  const {
    scope,
    profile,
    seed,
    artifactBundle,
    execute,
    actionExecutor,
    exactExecution,
    harness,
    schemaValidator,
    scenarioFilter,
    scenarioSelection,
    signal,
    declaredTargetLabel,
    prepareTimeoutMs,
    requestTimeoutMs,
    cleanupTimeoutMs,
  } = options;
  return Object.freeze({
    scope,
    profile,
    seed,
    artifactBundle,
    execute,
    harness,
    declaredTargetLabel,
    ...(exactExecution === undefined
      ? {}
      : { exactExecution: snapshotExactExecution(exactExecution) }),
    ...(schemaValidator === undefined ? {} : { schemaValidator }),
    ...(actionExecutor === undefined ? {} : { actionExecutor }),
    ...(scenarioFilter === undefined ? {} : { scenarioFilter }),
    ...(scenarioSelection === undefined
      ? {}
      : { scenarioSelection: Object.freeze([...scenarioSelection]) }),
    ...(signal === undefined ? {} : { signal }),
    ...(prepareTimeoutMs === undefined ? {} : { prepareTimeoutMs }),
    ...(requestTimeoutMs === undefined ? {} : { requestTimeoutMs }),
    ...(cleanupTimeoutMs === undefined ? {} : { cleanupTimeoutMs }),
  });
}

async function runScenario(
  metadata: ScenarioMetadata,
  scenario: ExecutableScenario,
  options: ScenarioRunOptions,
  context?: TransactionalRunContext,
): Promise<ScenarioRunResult> {
  if (metadata.requiredProfile !== scenario.requiredProfile)
    return result(
      metadata,
      scenario,
      "harness-error",
      "catalog and executable plan disagree on requiredProfile",
      [],
    );
  if (!profileIncludes(options.profile, scenario.requiredProfile))
    return {
      id: scenario.id,
      requiredProfile: scenario.requiredProfile,
      state: "unsupported-profile",
      requirements: metadata.requirements.map(({ source, anchor }) => `${source}${anchor}`),
      reason: "scenario requires a higher cumulative profile",
      exchanges: [],
    };
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const detach = relayAbort(options.signal, controller);
  const exchanges: ObservedExchange[] = [];
  const actions: ObservedAction[] = [];
  let fixturePreparationSettled = true;
  const actionSettlement: ActionSettlement = { settled: true, description: "HTTP exchange" };
  let runResult: ScenarioRunResult;
  try {
    if (controller.signal.aborted)
      throw new ConformanceRunnerError("scenario run was aborted before preparation");
    const prepareDeadline = createDeadlineSignal(options.prepareTimeoutMs ?? 30_000);
    const prepareSignal = AbortSignal.any([controller.signal, prepareDeadline.signal]);
    let preparation: FixturePreparation;
    fixturePreparationSettled = false;
    try {
      const preparationOperation = Promise.resolve()
        .then(() =>
          options.harness.prepare(
            scenario,
            options.scope,
            options.seed,
            options.artifactBundle.fixture,
            prepareSignal,
          ),
        )
        .then(
          (value) => {
            fixturePreparationSettled = true;
            return value;
          },
          (error: unknown) => {
            fixturePreparationSettled = true;
            throw error;
          },
        );
      preparation = snapshotFixturePreparation(
        await enforceDeadline(preparationOperation, prepareSignal, () =>
          controller.signal.aborted
            ? new ConformanceRunnerError("scenario run was aborted during preparation")
            : new ScenarioDeadlineError("fixture preparation timed out"),
        ),
      );
    } catch (error) {
      if (prepareDeadline.signal.aborted && !controller.signal.aborted)
        throw new ScenarioDeadlineError("fixture preparation timed out");
      throw error;
    } finally {
      prepareDeadline.clear();
    }
    validateFixturePreparation(preparation, options.artifactBundle.fixture, options.scope);
    const capabilities = new Set(preparation.capabilities);
    const missingSetup = scenario.setup.requires.filter(
      (capability) => !capabilities.has(capability),
    );
    const missingApplicability = scenario.applicability.requires.filter(
      (capability) => !capabilities.has(capability),
    );
    // An inapplicable scenario must not demand its bindings; every applicable
    // scenario — including one that will fail on a missing setup capability —
    // keeps full binding-flow validation.
    if (missingApplicability.length === 0)
      validateScenarioBindingFlow(scenario, preparation.bindings ?? {}, options.scope);
    if (missingSetup.length > 0) {
      runResult = result(
        metadata,
        scenario,
        "harness-error",
        `missing harness capabilities: ${missingSetup.join(", ")}`,
        exchanges,
      );
    } else if (missingApplicability.length > 0) {
      runResult = result(
        metadata,
        scenario,
        "not-applicable",
        `harness does not provide optional capabilities: ${missingApplicability.join(", ")}`,
        exchanges,
      );
    } else {
      const bindings = new Map<string, JsonValue>([
        ["scope", options.scope],
        ...Object.entries(preparation.bindings ?? {}),
      ]);
      const priorResponses = new Map<string, ComparableResponse>();
      const transactional =
        context === undefined
          ? undefined
          : createTransactionalObservations(
              context,
              scenario.id,
              options.scope,
              scenarioActionSequence(scenario).length,
            );
      for (const action of scenarioActionSequence(scenario)) {
        actionSettlement.settled = true;
        actionSettlement.description =
          action.family === "http" ? "HTTP exchange" : "action execution";
        try {
          await runScenarioAction(
            action,
            bindings,
            exchanges,
            actions,
            options.execute,
            options.actionExecutor,
            options.schemaValidator,
            options.artifactBundle.fixture,
            options.scope,
            options.profile,
            controller.signal,
            options.requestTimeoutMs ?? 30_000,
            actionSettlement,
            priorResponses,
            options,
            transactional,
          );
        } catch (error) {
          if (action.prerequisiteScenario !== undefined)
            throw new ScenarioPrerequisiteRecheckFailure(action.prerequisiteScenario, error);
          throw error;
        }
      }
      runResult = result(metadata, scenario, "pass", undefined, exchanges);
    }
  } catch (error) {
    if (controller.signal.aborted)
      runResult = result(
        metadata,
        scenario,
        "harness-error",
        "scenario run was aborted",
        exchanges,
        "aborted",
      );
    else if (error instanceof ScenarioPrerequisiteRecheckFailure)
      runResult = prerequisiteFailureResult(
        metadata,
        scenario,
        error,
        exchanges,
        options.artifactBundle.catalog,
      );
    else if (error instanceof ScenarioActionExecutorMissingError)
      runResult = result(
        metadata,
        scenario,
        "harness-error",
        error.message,
        exchanges,
        "not-implemented",
      );
    else if (error instanceof ScenarioAssertionFailure)
      runResult = result(metadata, scenario, "fail", error.message, exchanges);
    else if (error instanceof ScenarioActionExecutionFailure)
      runResult = result(metadata, scenario, "fail", error.message, exchanges);
    else if (error instanceof ScenarioUnsupportedProfileError)
      runResult = result(
        metadata,
        scenario,
        "unsupported-profile",
        "target advertised an unsupported profile",
        exchanges,
      );
    else if (error instanceof ScenarioOutOfScopeTargetError)
      runResult = result(
        metadata,
        scenario,
        "harness-error",
        error.message,
        exchanges,
        "out-of-scope-target",
      );
    else if (error instanceof TransactionalHarnessError)
      runResult = result(metadata, scenario, "harness-error", error.message, exchanges);
    else if (error instanceof ExactHarnessError)
      runResult = result(
        metadata,
        scenario,
        "harness-error",
        error.message,
        exchanges,
        error.category,
      );
    else if (
      error instanceof ScenarioObservationFailure ||
      error instanceof TransactionalReceiptFailure
    )
      runResult = result(metadata, scenario, "fail", error.message, exchanges);
    else if (error instanceof ScenarioObservationLimitError)
      runResult = result(
        metadata,
        scenario,
        "harness-error",
        error.message,
        exchanges,
        "observation-limit",
      );
    else if (error instanceof ScenarioWireObservationUnavailableError)
      runResult = result(
        metadata,
        scenario,
        "harness-error",
        error.message,
        exchanges,
        "wire-observation-unavailable",
      );
    else if (error instanceof HttpTransportError && error.category === "abort")
      runResult = result(
        metadata,
        scenario,
        "harness-error",
        "scenario run was aborted",
        exchanges,
        "aborted",
      );
    else if (error instanceof ScenarioDeadlineError)
      runResult = result(metadata, scenario, "harness-error", error.message, exchanges, "deadline");
    else if (error instanceof HttpTransportError && error.category === "configuration")
      runResult = result(
        metadata,
        scenario,
        "harness-error",
        "HTTP executor configuration failed",
        exchanges,
      );
    else if (error instanceof HttpTransportError) {
      const harnessCategory =
        error.category === "timeout"
          ? ("deadline" as const)
          : isTransportObservationLimit(error.category)
            ? ("observation-limit" as const)
            : undefined;
      runResult = result(
        metadata,
        scenario,
        harnessCategory === undefined ? "fail" : "harness-error",
        transportFailureMessage(error.category),
        exchanges,
        harnessCategory,
      );
    } else if (error instanceof ConformanceRunnerError)
      runResult = result(metadata, scenario, "harness-error", error.message, exchanges);
    else
      runResult = result(metadata, scenario, "harness-error", "scenario harness failed", exchanges);
  }
  if (actions.length > 0) runResult = { ...runResult, actions };
  if (!fixturePreparationSettled || !actionSettlement.settled) {
    detach();
    return {
      ...runResult,
      cleanupError: !fixturePreparationSettled
        ? "cleanup skipped because fixture preparation did not settle"
        : `cleanup skipped because an ${actionSettlement.description} did not settle`,
    };
  }
  const cleanupRemaining =
    actionSettlement.cleanupEndsAt === undefined
      ? (options.cleanupTimeoutMs ?? 10_000)
      : actionSettlement.cleanupEndsAt - performance.now();
  if (cleanupRemaining <= 0) {
    detach();
    return { ...runResult, cleanupError: "cleanup budget exhausted collecting exact settlement" };
  }
  const cleanupDeadline = createDeadlineSignal(cleanupRemaining);
  const cleanupSignal = cleanupDeadline.signal;
  try {
    await enforceDeadline(
      options.harness.cleanup(scenario, options.scope, cleanupSignal),
      cleanupSignal,
      () => new ScenarioDeadlineError("fixture cleanup timed out"),
    );
  } catch {
    const timedOut = cleanupDeadline.signal.aborted;
    runResult = {
      ...runResult,
      ...(runResult.state === "pass" ? { state: "harness-error" as const } : {}),
      ...(runResult.category === undefined
        ? { category: timedOut ? ("deadline" as const) : ("cleanup" as const) }
        : {}),
      cleanupError: timedOut ? "cleanup timed out" : "cleanup failed",
    };
  } finally {
    cleanupDeadline.clear();
    detach();
  }
  return runResult;
}
