===== packages/server/src/transaction-store.ts whole-file SHA256 fcd6882b29e538db45867c894c06dfbaa6ca588db0c2e403ef1b7abb14f6c706 complete named block, starts line 1134 =====
  function executeClaim(claim: TransactionClaim): TransactionReceipt;
  function executeClaim(claim: TransactionClaim, observed: true): TransactionReceiptObservation;
  function executeClaim(
    claim: TransactionClaim,
    observed = false,
  ): TransactionReceipt | TransactionReceiptObservation {
    return enter(() => {
      const owned = claims.get(claim);
      if (!owned) throw new Error("foreign transaction claim");
      try {
        return atomic(() => {
          const current = row(owned.principal, owned.key);
          if (current?.owner !== owned.owner) throw new Error("transaction execution fenced");
          const normalized = owned.normalized;
          if (!normalized || current.dependency) throw new Error("unresolved execution");
          const core = parseReceipt(current.receipt);
          const previous = Number(metadata("position"));
          if (
            !Number.isSafeInteger(previous) ||
            previous < 0 ||
            previous === Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
          )
            throw new Error("position allocation unsafe");
          const position = `pos-${previous + 1}`,
            now = instant();
          let active = true;
          function scoped<T>(fn: () => T): T {
            if (!active) throw new Error("expired Resource transaction");
            return fn();
          }
          const tx: TransactionMutationContext = {
            resources: (kind) =>
              scoped(() =>
                Object.freeze(
                  db
                    .prepare("SELECT id FROM resources WHERE kind=?")
                    .all(kind)
                    .map((row) => Object.freeze(requiredResource(String(row.id)))),
                ),
              ),
            resource: (id) => scoped(() => resource(id)),
            incidentLinks: (id) =>
              scoped(() =>
                db
                  .prepare("SELECT id FROM resources WHERE kind='link' AND (source=? OR target=?)")
                  .all(id, id)
                  .map((r) => requiredResource(String(r.id))),
              ),
            outgoingLinks: (id) =>
              scoped(() =>
                db
                  .prepare("SELECT id FROM resources WHERE kind='link' AND source=?")
                  .all(id)
                  .map((r) => requiredResource(String(r.id))),
              ),
            identityWasCommitted: (id) =>
              scoped(
                () => db.prepare("SELECT id FROM identities WHERE id=?").get(id) !== undefined,
              ),
            alias: (path) => scoped(() => alias(path)),
            putAlias: (path, target) =>
              scoped(() => {
                db.prepare(
                  "INSERT INTO aliases VALUES(?,?) ON CONFLICT(path) DO UPDATE SET target=excluded.target",
                ).run(path, target);
              }),
            deleteAlias: (path) =>
              scoped(() => {
                db.prepare("DELETE FROM aliases WHERE path=?").run(path);
              }),
            installedType: (id) => scoped(() => installed.get(id)),
            allocateResourceId: (kind) =>
              scoped(() => {
                for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 32; attempt++) {
                  const suffix = randomUUID(),
                    id = `${kind === "bead" ? "beads" : "links"}/${suffix}`;
                  if (
                    !db.prepare("SELECT id FROM identities WHERE id=?").get(id) &&
                    (kind !== "bead" || alias(suffix) === undefined)
                  )
                    return id;
                }
                throw new Error("Resource identity allocation exhausted");
              }),
            allocateRevision: () => scoped(() => randomUUID()),
            putResource: (r) =>
              scoped(() => {
                db.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO identities VALUES(?)").run(r.id);
                db.prepare(
                  "INSERT INTO resources VALUES(?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET body=excluded.body",
                ).run(r.id, r.bodyJson, r.kind, r.source ?? null, r.target ?? null);
              }),
            deleteResource: (id) =>
              scoped(() => {
                // Retain its identity for the epoch; deletion never permits reuse.
                db.prepare("DELETE FROM resources WHERE id=?").run(id);
              }),
          };
          db.exec("SAVEPOINT effects");
          const result = (() => {
            try {
              return evaluateTransaction(
                normalized,
                tx,
                {
                  scope,
                  contracts: compiled?.registry ?? {
                    get(id, bytes) {
                      const descriptor = descriptors.find((d) => d.id === id);
                      return descriptor && installed.get(id) === bytes ? { descriptor } : undefined;
                    },
                  },
                  aliasPolicy: { canRead: () => true, canWriteBead: () => true },
                  policy: { canRead: () => true, canCreate: () => true, canWrite: () => true },
                  maximumEndpointMultiplicity: [],
                  observeCommitTime: instant,
                  recordChangeContext: false,
                  limits: TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS,
                },
                core.transaction,
                position,
                now,
              );
            } finally {
              active = false;
            }
          })();
          options.fault?.("after-effects");
          // The minimum starts when evaluation has become terminal, not at
          // admission or before Resource callbacks have consumed execution time.
          const expiresAt = new Date(instant() + day).toISOString();
          let receipt: TransactionReceipt;
          if (!result.ok) {
            db.exec("ROLLBACK TO effects; RELEASE effects");
            receipt = {
              ...core,
              status: "failed",
              detail: "available",
              requiredPosition: `pos-${previous}`,
              problem: result.problem,
              expiresAt,
            };
          } else {
            db.exec("RELEASE effects");
            const group = {
              scopeEpoch: epoch,
              authorizationView: view,
              checkpoint: position,
              position,
              previousPosition: `pos-${previous}`,
              projectionAdvance: false,
              transaction: core.transaction,
              changes: result.changes,
              erasures: [],
              eventCount: result.events.length,
              events: result.events,
            };
            const effective = result.events.length > 0;
            if (effective) {
              db.prepare("INSERT INTO groups VALUES(?,?)").run(previous + 1, json(group));
              db.prepare("UPDATE metadata SET value=? WHERE name='position'").run(
                String(previous + 1),
              );
            }
            receipt = {
              ...core,
              status: "completed",
              detail: "available",
              requiredPosition: effective ? position : `pos-${previous}`,
              ...(effective ? { effectPosition: position } : {}),
              results: result.results,
              next: null,
              expiresAt,
            };
          }
          const changed = db
            .prepare(
              "UPDATE receipts SET receipt=?,owner=NULL WHERE principal=? AND key=? AND owner=?",
            )
            .run(json(receipt), owned.principal, owned.key, owned.owner);
          if (changed.changes !== 1) throw new Error("terminal ownership lost");
          if (owned.creator)
            writeFact(
              owned.creator.attempt,
              owned.creator.member,
              creatorFact(owned.creator.operation, receipt),
            );
          if (receipt.status === "completed") createPagePlan(owned.principal, owned.key, receipt);
          options.fault?.("after-terminal");
          return observed ? observeReceipt(receipt) : receipt;
        });
      } catch (error) {
        // A live internal/transient abort retracts only this execution's pending
        // reservation. An abrupt process death is handled by startup instead.
        cleanupThenThrow(error, () => {
          atomic(() => {
            db.prepare("DELETE FROM receipts WHERE principal=? AND key=? AND owner=?").run(
              owned.principal,
              owned.key,
              owned.owner,
            );
          });
        });
      } finally {
        claims.delete(claim);
      }
    });
  }


===== packages/server/src/transaction-batch.ts whole-file SHA256 70d941d416979edd54e250d51318cfb7e5d4ddb315ea6131de7ca5f56e98a014 complete named block, starts line 540 =====
export interface TransactionEvent {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly ordinal: number;
  readonly type: "created" | "updated" | "deleted" | "linked" | "unlinked";
  readonly source: string;
  readonly subject: string;
  readonly subjectType: string;
  readonly transaction: string;
  readonly time: string;
  readonly data: unknown;
}
export interface TransactionStateChange {
  readonly operation: "upsert" | "tombstone";
  readonly resourceKind: "bead" | "link";
  readonly resource:
    | ResourceRecord
    | { readonly id: string; readonly type: string; readonly revision: string };
}
// Source-private receipt subset; shared Read+Update Problems also describe
// receiving/Read failures that are not terminal mutation receipt outcomes.
const receiptProblemCodes = [
  "forbidden",
  "resource-not-found",
  "limit-exceeded",
  "binding-unavailable",
  "validation-failed",
  "type-not-installed",
  "identity-taken",
  "alias-path-taken",
  "revision-mismatch",
  "incident-links-exist",
  "aggregate-constraint-violation",
  "cardinality-violated",
  "revision-allocation-unsafe",
] as const;
type ReceiptProblemCode = (typeof receiptProblemCodes)[number];
const receiptProblemStatuses = [400, 403, 404, 409, 413, 422] as const;
type ReceiptProblemStatus = (typeof receiptProblemStatuses)[number];
function isReceiptCode(value: unknown): value is ReceiptProblemCode {
  return receiptProblemCodes.some((code) => code === value);
}
function isReceiptStatus(value: unknown): value is ReceiptProblemStatus {
  return receiptProblemStatuses.some((status) => status === value);
}
// Restate required receipt fields: mapped Omit over RFC9457's string index
// signature would erase their requirements. Preserve extension fields explicitly.
interface BatchProblem {
  readonly type: string;
  readonly status: ReceiptProblemStatus;
  readonly retry: ReadUpdateProblem["retry"];
  readonly diagnostics?: ReadUpdateProblem["diagnostics"];
  readonly diagnosticsTruncated?: boolean;
  readonly [key: string]: unknown;
}
function receiptProblem(
  problem: ReadUpdateProblem,
): BatchProblem & { readonly code: ReceiptProblemCode } {
  if (
    typeof problem.type !== "string" ||
    !isReceiptCode(problem.code) ||
    !isReceiptStatus(problem.status) ||
    !["never", "after-state-change", "after-delay"].includes(problem.retry)
  )
    throw new Error("evaluator failure lacks required receipt Problem fields");
  return { ...problem, type: problem.type, code: problem.code, status: problem.status };
}
export type BatchEvaluation =
  | {
      readonly ok: true;
      readonly results: readonly unknown[];
      readonly changes: readonly TransactionStateChange[];
      readonly events: readonly TransactionEvent[];
    }
  | {
      readonly ok: false;
      readonly problem: BatchProblem & {
        readonly code: ReceiptProblemCode;
        readonly operationIndex: number;
        readonly operationName?: string;
      };
    }
  | {
      readonly ok: false;
      readonly problem: BatchProblem & {
        readonly code: "limit-exceeded";
        readonly limit: "transaction.inducedEvents" | "transaction.matchedResources";
        readonly operationIndex?: never;
        readonly operationName?: never;
      };
    };
export type TransactionMutationContext = ResourceTransaction &
  AliasTransaction & {
    resources(
      kind: "bead" | "link",
    ): readonly NonNullable<ReturnType<ResourceTransaction["resource"]>>[];
  };
export interface TransactionEvaluationOptions extends ResourceEvaluationOptions {
  /** TX materialization proof requires finite complete-Resource bounds.
   * The owner passes the captured tuple; this does not change RU optionality. */
  readonly limits: ResourceEvaluationOptions["limits"] & {
    readonly propertiesBytes: number;
    readonly representationBytes: number;
    readonly diagnosticBytes: number;
    readonly diagnosticCount: number;
  };
  readonly aliasPolicy: AliasEvaluationOptions["policy"];
  /** Evaluator-only test seam; cannot raise the private authority cap. */
  readonly maximumInducedEvents?: number;
  readonly maximumMatchedResources?: number;
}
export function evaluateTransaction(
  normalized: NormalizedTransaction,
  tx: TransactionMutationContext,
  options: TransactionEvaluationOptions,
  transaction: string,
  position: string,
  now: number,
): BatchEvaluation {
  for (const field of [
    "propertiesBytes",
    "representationBytes",
    "diagnosticBytes",
    "diagnosticCount",
  ] as const) {
    const value = options.limits[field];
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 1 || value > TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS[field])
      throw new TypeError(`invalid transaction evaluator ${field} bound`);
  }
  const state = normalizedState.get(normalized);
  if (!state || state.batch.carrier.scope !== options.scope)
    throw new TypeError("expected normalized transaction for this Scope");
  const eventMaximum = options.maximumInducedEvents ?? TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.inducedEvents;
  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(eventMaximum) ||
    eventMaximum < 1 ||
    eventMaximum > TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.inducedEvents
  )
    throw new TypeError("invalid evaluator Event bound");
  const matchMaximum =
    options.maximumMatchedResources ?? TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.matchedResources;
  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(matchMaximum) ||
    matchMaximum < 1 ||
    matchMaximum > TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.matchedResources
  )
    throw new TypeError("invalid evaluator matched Resource bound");
  let matchedResources = 0;
  const batch = state.batch;
  const bindings = new Map<string, string>();
  const results: unknown[] = [],
    events: TransactionEvent[] = [];
  const changes = new Map<string, TransactionStateChange>();
  function scopeEvent(subject: ResourceRecord, type: TransactionEvent["type"], data: unknown) {
    if (events.length >= eventMaximum) throw new EventExpansionLimit();
    const ordinal = events.length;
    events.push({
      id: `${position}-${ordinal}`,
      ordinal,
      type,
      // These are authority Scope-group facts, not Resource-source projections.
      // The latter rewrite source to `${resource.id}?view=events` on read.
      source: `${options.scope}events/`,
      subject: subject.id,
      subjectType: subject.type,
      transaction,
      time: new Date(now).toISOString(),
      data,
    });
  }
  function applyResource(
    op: { readonly operation: ResourceOperation; readonly name?: string },
    input: Record<string, unknown>,
    operationIndex: number,
    aliases: ReadonlyMap<string, string | null>,
  ): Extract<BatchEvaluation, { ok: false }> | undefined {
    const locator = input.bead ?? input.link;
    const beforeRow =
      typeof locator === "string"
        ? tx.resource(
            locator.startsWith(options.scope) ? locator.slice(options.scope.length) : locator,
          )
        : undefined;
    const before = beforeRow ? readStoredResource(beforeRow, options.scope) : undefined;
    // Capture each same-turn preimage before the evaluator writes either the
    // direct Resource or its owning source. No recipe or allocator is duplicated.
    const previous = new Map<string, ResourceRecord | undefined>();
    // Syntax and numeric admission preserve the operation/input pairing.
    const result = evaluateResourceMutation(
      {
        ...tx,
        putResource(row) {
          if (!previous.has(row.id)) {
            const old = tx.resource(row.id);
            previous.set(row.id, old ? readStoredResource(old, options.scope) : undefined);
          }
          tx.putResource(row);
        },
        // A captured miss must not become a live target after admission. Other
        // lookups (e.g. creation uniqueness) still consult current authority state.
        alias(path) {
          const locator = `${options.scope}alias/${path}`;
          if (!aliases.has(locator)) return tx.alias(path);
          const target = aliases.get(locator);
          return target == null ? undefined : target.slice(options.scope.length);
        },
      },
      { operation: op.operation, input } as ResourceMutation,
      options,
    );
    if (result.effect === "failure")
      return {
        ok: false,
        problem: {
          ...receiptProblem(result.outcome),
          operationIndex,
          ...(op.name ? { operationName: op.name } : {}),
        },
      };
    const outcome = result.outcome;
    results.push({ operationIndex, ...(op.name ? { operationName: op.name } : {}), ...outcome });
    const resource = outcome.resource;
    if (op.name && outcome.outcome === "created" && resource) bindings.set(op.name, resource.id);
    // A retained revision is an observed no-op, not a committed transition.
    if (result.changed.length === 0 && result.deleted.length === 0) return;
    const subject = resource ?? before;
    if (!subject) throw new Error("successful transition lacks Resource state");
    // Evaluator IDs are local lookup paths; group identity is always canonical.
    // One owned operation changes at most the Link and its source Bead.
    for (const id of result.changed) {
      const row = tx.resource(id);
      if (!row) throw new Error("changed Resource lacks staged postimage");
      const record = readStoredResource(row, options.scope);
      changes.set(record.id, {
        operation: "upsert",
        resourceKind: "source" in record ? "link" : "bead",
        resource: record,
      });
    }
    for (const id of result.deleted) {
      if (
        !before ||
        !outcome.deleted ||
        before.id !== `${options.scope}${id}` ||
        outcome.deleted.resource.id !== before.id ||
        outcome.deleted.resource.type !== before.type ||
        outcome.deleted.resource.revision !== before.revision
      )
        throw new Error("deleted Resource lacks matching previous identity");
      changes.set(before.id, { operation: "tombstone", ...outcome.deleted });
    }
    if (outcome.outcome === "created") {
      scopeEvent(subject, "created", {
        revision: subject.revision,
        properties: subject.properties,
        metadata: subject.metadata ?? {},
        ...(subject.revisionProvenance ? { revisionProvenance: subject.revisionProvenance } : {}),
        ...("source" in subject ? { source: subject.source, target: subject.target } : {}),
      });
    } else if (outcome.outcome === "updated") {
      if (!before) throw new Error("update lacks previous state");
      scopeEvent(subject, "updated", {
        previousRevision: before.revision,
        revision: subject.revision,
        ...(input.propertiesChange !== undefined ? { change: input.propertiesChange } : {}),
        ...(input.metadataChange !== undefined ? { metadataChange: input.metadataChange } : {}),
        ...(subject.revisionProvenance ? { revisionProvenance: subject.revisionProvenance } : {}),
      });
    } else {
      scopeEvent(subject, "deleted", { revision: subject.revision });
    }
    if ("source" in subject && outcome.outcome !== "updated") {
      for (const endpoint of ["source", "target"] as const) {
        const uri = referenceUri(subject[endpoint]);
        if (!uri.startsWith(options.scope)) continue;
        if (!tx.resource(uri.slice(options.scope.length)))
          throw new Error("missing staged endpoint");
        scopeEvent(subject, outcome.outcome === "deleted" ? "unlinked" : "linked", {
          endpoint,
          link: { id: subject.id, type: subject.type },
          source: subject.source,
          target: subject.target,
        });
      }
    }
    if (outcome.sourceRevision !== undefined) {
      if (!("source" in subject) || !outcome.source?.startsWith(options.scope))
        throw new Error("owned transition lacks canonical Link/source identity");
      const localSource = outcome.source.slice(options.scope.length);
      const oldSource = previous.get(localSource),
        row = tx.resource(localSource);
      const source = row ? readStoredResource(row, options.scope) : undefined;
      if (
        !oldSource ||
        !source ||
        "source" in source ||
        "source" in oldSource ||
        source.id !== outcome.source ||
        source.revision !== outcome.sourceRevision
      )
        throw new Error("owned transition lacks matching source versions");
      let link: unknown;
      if (outcome.outcome === "created") link = subject;
      else if (outcome.outcome === "deleted")
        link = { id: subject.id, type: subject.type, revision: subject.revision };
      else {
        if (!before || (input.propertiesChange === undefined && input.metadataChange === undefined))
          throw new Error("owned update lacks committed patch");
        link = {
          id: subject.id,
          type: subject.type,
          previousRevision: before.revision,
          revision: subject.revision,
          ...(input.propertiesChange !== undefined ? { change: input.propertiesChange } : {}),
          ...(input.metadataChange !== undefined ? { metadataChange: input.metadataChange } : {}),
          ...(subject.revisionProvenance ? { revisionProvenance: subject.revisionProvenance } : {}),
        };
      }
      // Up to four facts: Link lifecycle, source/target incidence, owning Bead.
      // The source Event is last and never carries a direct Resource patch.
      scopeEvent(source, "updated", {
        previousRevision: oldSource.revision,
        revision: source.revision,
        ownedLink: { operation: outcome.outcome, link },
        ...(source.revisionProvenance ? { revisionProvenance: source.revisionProvenance } : {}),
      });
    }
    return undefined;
  }
  try {
    for (const [operationIndex, op] of batch.carrier.operations.entries()) {
      if ([...state.references.values()].includes(UNBOUND_SEMANTIC_REFERENCE))
        return {
          ok: false,
          problem: {
            type: "https://github.com/gastownhall/bdp/problems/request",
            status: 400,
            code: "binding-unavailable",
            retry: "never",
            operationIndex,
          },
        };
      if (op.operation === "updateWhere" || op.operation === "deleteWhere") {
        const admitted = admitJsonNumbers(op.input, {
          diagnostics: TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.diagnosticCount,
          diagnosticBytes: TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.diagnosticBytes,
          diagnostic: ({ pointer }) => ({
            message: `unsupported number at request location ${pointer}`,
          }),
        });
        if (!admitted.ok)
          return {
            ok: false,
            problem: {
              type: "https://github.com/gastownhall/bdp/problems/validation",
              status: 422,
              code: "validation-failed",
              retry: "never",
              operationIndex,
              pointer: `${batch.carrier.kind === "singleton" ? "" : `/operations/${operationIndex}`}${admitted.offending[0]!.pointer}`,
              diagnostics: admitted.diagnostics,
              ...(admitted.diagnosticsTruncated ? { diagnosticsTruncated: true } : {}),
            },
          };
        const input = admitted.value as Record<string, unknown>;
        const selected = selectReadResources(
          input.selector as string,
          TRANSACTION_SELECTOR_LIMITS,
          tx
            .resources(input.collection === "beads" ? "bead" : "link")
            .map((row) => {
              if (!row) throw new Error("staged collection contains missing Resource");
              return readStoredResource(row, options.scope);
            })
            .filter((record) => isResourceVisible(tx, record, options.scope, options.policy)),
        )
          .slice()
          .sort((a, b) => compareCanonicalIds(a.id, b.id));
        const cardinality = input.cardinality as { min?: number; max?: number } | undefined;
        if (
          (cardinality?.min !== undefined && selected.length < cardinality.min) ||
          (cardinality?.max !== undefined && selected.length > cardinality.max)
        )
          return {
            ok: false,
            problem: {
              type: "https://github.com/gastownhall/bdp/problems/conflict",
              status: 409,
              code: "cardinality-violated",
              retry: "after-state-change",
              operationIndex,
              pointer: `${batch.carrier.kind === "singleton" ? "" : `/operations/${operationIndex}`}/cardinality/${cardinality?.min !== undefined && selected.length < cardinality.min ? "min" : "max"}`,
            },
          };
        // Count selections across operations, including repeat matches and
        // semantic no-ops, before adding their result entries. Enumeration and
        // selection above still scan the complete candidate collection.
        matchedResources += selected.length;
        if (matchedResources > matchMaximum) throw new MatchExpansionLimit();
        results.push({ operationIndex, outcome: "matched", count: selected.length });
        for (const resource of selected) {
          const bead = input.collection === "beads";
          const failure = applyResource(
            {
              operation:
                op.operation === "updateWhere"
                  ? bead
                    ? "updateBead"
                    : "updateLink"
                  : bead
                    ? "deleteBead"
                    : "deleteLink",
            },
            {
              [bead ? "bead" : "link"]: resource.id,
              ...(op.operation === "updateWhere" ? { propertiesChange: input.change } : {}),
              ...(input.revisionProvenance === undefined
                ? {}
                : { revisionProvenance: input.revisionProvenance }),
            },
            operationIndex,
            new Map(),
          );
          if (failure) {
            if (op.operation === "updateWhere" && failure.problem.code === "validation-failed") {
              // Diagnostics describe the final properties document, not the request.
              // Only an actual Property Change path failure identifies a path member;
              // repeated paths (or afterstate validation) identify the array, not an index.
              let location = "/change";
              const diagnostic = failure.problem.diagnostics?.[0];
              if (
                diagnostic?.message.startsWith("Property Change ") &&
                Array.isArray(input.change)
              ) {
                const matches = input.change.flatMap((change, index) =>
                  change.path === diagnostic.instanceLocation ? [index] : [],
                );
                if (matches.length === 1) location += `/${matches[0]}/path`;
              }
              return {
                ...failure,
                problem: {
                  ...failure.problem,
                  pointer: `${batch.carrier.kind === "singleton" ? "" : `/operations/${operationIndex}`}${location}`,
                },
              };
            }
            return failure;
          }
        }
        continue;
      }
      const admitted = admitReadUpdateOperationNumbers(
        op as UnadmittedReadUpdateOperation,
        memberNumericDiagnosticBudget(op.operation, {
          diagnostics: TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.diagnosticCount,
          diagnosticBytes: TRANSACTION_RUNTIME_LIMITS.diagnosticBytes,
          diagnostic: ({ pointer }) => ({
            message:
              pointer.startsWith("/propertiesChange/") || pointer.startsWith("/metadataChange/")
                ? `unsupported number at request location ${pointer}`
                : "unsupported number",
          }),
        }),
      );
      if (!admitted.ok)
        return {
          ok: false,
          problem: {
            type: "https://github.com/gastownhall/bdp/problems/validation",
            status: 422,
            code: "validation-failed",
            retry: "never",
            diagnostics: admitted.diagnostics,
            ...(admitted.diagnosticsTruncated ? { diagnosticsTruncated: true } : {}),
            operationIndex,
            ...(op.name ? { operationName: op.name } : {}),
          },
        };
      const input: Record<string, unknown> = { ...admitted.input };
      if (operationIndex === 0)
        for (const [field, value] of state.references) {
          const prior = input[field];
          input[field] = typeof prior === "string" ? value : { ...(prior as object), uri: value };
        }
      if (op.operation === "putAlias" || op.operation === "deleteAlias") {
        // Alias singletons and sequence members share this one-operation owner.
        // Locator effects have
        // a durable receipt but deliberately no Resource Event or Scope position.
        if (batch.carrier.kind !== "singleton" || batch.carrier.operations.length !== 1)
          throw new Error("alias operation escaped singleton admission");
        const result = evaluateAliasMutation(
          tx,
          { operation: op.operation, input } as AliasMutation,
          { scope: options.scope, policy: options.aliasPolicy, limits: options.limits },
        );
        if (result.effect === "failure")
          return { ok: false, problem: { ...receiptProblem(result.outcome), operationIndex } };
        results.push({ operationIndex, ...result.outcome });
        continue;
      }
      const aliases = state.aliases[operationIndex];
      if (!aliases) throw new Error("missing admitted operation witnesses");
      for (const field of ["source", "target", "bead", "link"]) {
        const ref = input[field] as string | { uri: string; revision: string } | undefined;
        if (ref === undefined) continue;
        const uri = referenceUri(ref);
        if (uri.startsWith("@")) {
          const id = bindings.get(uri.slice(1));
          if (!id) throw new Error("validated binding missing");
          input[field] = typeof ref === "string" ? id : { ...ref, uri: id };
        } else if (transactionAliasFields(op.operation).includes(field)) {
          const target = aliases.get(canonicalReference(options.scope, uri));
          if (target != null)
            input[field] = typeof ref === "string" ? target : { ...ref, uri: target };
        }
      }
      const failure = applyResource(
        { operation: op.operation as ResourceOperation, ...(op.name ? { name: op.name } : {}) },
        input,
        operationIndex,
        aliases,
      );
      if (failure) return failure;
    }
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof EventExpansionLimit) && !(error instanceof MatchExpansionLimit))
      throw error;
    return {
      ok: false,
      problem: {
        type: "https://github.com/gastownhall/bdp/problems/size",
        status: 413,
        code: "limit-exceeded",
        retry: "never",
        limit:
          error instanceof MatchExpansionLimit
            ? "transaction.matchedResources"
            : "transaction.inducedEvents",
      },
    };
  }

  return { ok: true, results, changes: [...changes.values()], events };
}


===== packages/server/src/resource-evaluator.ts whole-file SHA256 89606787e455f4ed555b807d91c5331a376431807a9a581de1f94e538fcab4ff complete named block, starts line 602 =====
function evaluate(
  tx: ResourceTransaction,
  mutation: ResourceMutation,
  options: ResourceEvaluationOptions,
  fault: DiagnosticFault,
): ResourceEvaluation {
  const scope = options.scope;
  const { operation, input } = mutation;
  const resolutions: ResourceReferenceResolution[] = [];
  const creating = operation === "createBead" || operation === "createLink";
  const deleting = operation === "deleteBead" || operation === "deleteLink";
  const resourceKind: ResourceKind = operation.includes("Bead") ? "bead" : "link";
  const allocatedText = (value: unknown, kind: "identity" | "revision"): string => {
    if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length === 0)
      throw new TypeError(`allocator must return a nonempty ${kind} string`);
    return value;
  };
  const allocateRevision = (): string => {
    const value = tx.allocateRevision();
    // Only an established revision-allocation conflict has this wire meaning.
    // Broken facade values and Resource-ID allocator faults remain internal.
    if (typeof value === "object" && value !== null && value.kind === "allocation-unsafe")
      fail("revision-allocation-unsafe");
    return allocatedText(value, "revision");
  };
  const resolve = (spelling: string, expected: ResourceKind): string => {
    const uri = canonical(scope, spelling);
    const path = local(scope, uri);
    if (expected === "bead" && path?.startsWith("alias/")) {
      const target = tx.alias(path.slice(6));
      if (!target) fail("resource-not-found");
      return new URL(target, scope).href;
    }
    return uri;
  };
  const loadVisible = (uri: string, expected: ResourceKind): ResourceRecord => {
    const id = resourcePath(scope, uri, expected);
    const stored = id === undefined ? undefined : tx.resource(id);
    if (!stored || stored.kind !== expected) fail("resource-not-found");
    const record = storedRecord(stored, scope);
    if (!isResourceVisible(tx, record, scope, options.policy)) fail("resource-not-found");
    return record;
  };
  let id: string;
  let before: ResourceRecord | undefined;
  if (creating) {
    const supplied = "id" in input ? input.id : undefined;
    const spelling = supplied ?? allocatedText(tx.allocateResourceId(resourceKind), "identity");
    const path = resourcePath(scope, canonical(scope, spelling), resourceKind);
    if (path === undefined)
      throw new TypeError("creation ID must already have canonical Resource grammar and kind");
    if (supplied === undefined && path.indexOf("/", 6) !== -1)
      throw new Error("allocator must return one opaque Resource identity segment");
    id = path;
    if (supplied !== undefined)
      resolutions.push({
        inputPointer: "/id",
        original: supplied,
        resolved: new URL(id, scope).href,
      });
    if (tx.identityWasCommitted(id)) {
      if (supplied === undefined)
        throw new Error("allocator returned a committed Resource identity");
      fail("identity-taken");
    }
    if (resourceKind === "bead" && tx.alias(id.slice(6)) !== undefined) {
      if (supplied === undefined) throw new Error("allocator returned a live Bead alias path");
      fail("alias-path-taken");
    }
  } else {
    const subject = "bead" in input ? input.bead : "link" in input ? input.link : undefined;
    if (subject === undefined) throw new TypeError("Resource subject required");
    const resolved = resolve(subject, resourceKind);
    before = loadVisible(resolved, resourceKind);
    resolutions.push({
      inputPointer: resourceKind === "bead" ? "/bead" : "/link",
      original: subject,
      resolved,
    });
    id = local(scope, before.id) as string;
  }
  const uri = new URL(id, scope).href;
  const contractMap = new Map<string, InstalledResourceContract>();
  const contracts = (type: string): readonly InstalledResourceContract[] => {
    const pending = [type];
    const gathered = new Map<string, InstalledResourceContract>();
    while (pending.length) {
      const name = pending.pop();
      if (!name || gathered.has(name)) continue;
      let entry = contractMap.get(name);
      if (!entry) {
        const artifact = tx.installedType(name);
        const compiled = artifact === undefined ? undefined : options.contracts.get(name, artifact);
        if (!compiled) fail("type-not-installed");
        let descriptor: TypeDescriptor;
        try {
          descriptor = parseTypeDescriptor(compiled.descriptor);
        } catch {
          return fail("type-not-installed");
        }
        if (
          descriptor.id !== name ||
          (descriptor.propertiesSchema !== undefined && compiled.validateProperties === undefined)
        )
          fail("type-not-installed");
        entry = { ...compiled, descriptor };
        contractMap.set(name, entry);
      }
      gathered.set(name, entry);
      pending.push(...entry.descriptor.conformsTo);
    }
    try {
      createTypeConformanceIndex([...gathered.values()].map((entry) => entry.descriptor));
    } catch {
      return fail("type-not-installed");
    }
    return [...gathered.values()];
  };
  const type = "type" in input ? input.type : before?.type;
  if (type === undefined) throw new TypeError("Resource Type required");
  let source: BeadRecord | undefined;
  let endpoints: { source: Reference; target: Reference } | undefined;
  const endpoint = (
    reference: string | { readonly uri: string; readonly revision: string },
  ): Reference => {
    const spelling = typeof reference === "string" ? reference : reference.uri;
    const absolute = resolve(spelling, "bead");
    const path = local(scope, absolute);
    if (path !== undefined) {
      if (!resourcePath(scope, absolute, "bead"))
        bad("Link endpoint is not a Bead reference", options);
      loadVisible(absolute, "bead");
    }
    return (
      typeof reference === "string" ? absolute : { uri: absolute, revision: reference.revision }
    ) as Reference;
  };
  if (resourceKind === "link") {
    if (operation === "createLink")
      endpoints = { source: endpoint(input.source), target: endpoint(input.target) };
    else if (before && "source" in before)
      endpoints = { source: endpoint(before.source), target: endpoint(before.target) };
    if (!endpoints) throw new TypeError("Link endpoints required");
    if (
      !deleting &&
      local(scope, referenceUri(endpoints.source)) === undefined &&
      local(scope, referenceUri(endpoints.target)) === undefined
    )
      bad("a Link must have at least one in-Scope endpoint", options);

    if (operation === "createLink") {
      resolutions.push(
        { inputPointer: "/source", original: input.source, resolved: endpoints.source },
        { inputPointer: "/target", original: input.target, resolved: endpoints.target },
      );
    }
  }
  const effective = contracts(type);
  const declared = effective.find((entry) => entry.descriptor.id === type)?.descriptor;
  if (!declared) fail("type-not-installed");
  if (declared.describes !== resourceKind)
    bad("declared Type describes another Resource category", options);

  // Resolve installed endpoint contracts before value validation, while keeping
  // generic endpoint existence/visibility above and constraint diagnostics below
  // effective properties (spec: staged Resource validation).
  const endpointChecks: {
    type: string;
    side: "source" | "target";
    constraint: Extract<TypeDescriptor, { describes: "link" }>["source"];
    address: string;
    actual: readonly InstalledResourceContract[] | undefined;
  }[] = [];
  if (endpoints) {
    for (const entry of effective) {
      if (entry.descriptor.describes !== "link") fail("type-not-installed");
      for (const side of ["source", "target"] as const) {
        const constraint = entry.descriptor[side];
        const address = referenceUri(endpoints[side]);
        const required = constraint.conformsTo.flatMap((required) => [...contracts(required)]);
        if (required.some((contract) => contract.descriptor.describes !== "bead"))
          fail("type-not-installed");
        const bead = local(scope, address) === undefined ? undefined : loadVisible(address, "bead");
        endpointChecks.push({
          type: entry.descriptor.id,
          side,
          constraint,
          address,
          actual: bead === undefined ? undefined : contracts(bead.type),
        });
      }
    }
    const sourceId = local(scope, referenceUri(endpoints.source));
    if (sourceId !== undefined)
      source = loadVisible(new URL(sourceId, scope).href, "bead") as BeadRecord;
  }
  const properties = deleting
    ? (before?.properties ?? {})
    : "propertiesChange" in input && input.propertiesChange !== undefined
      ? patch(before?.properties ?? {}, input.propertiesChange, "properties", options)
      : "properties" in input
        ? (input.properties ?? {})
        : (before?.properties ?? {});
  const commonMetadata = deleting
    ? (before?.metadata ?? {})
    : "metadataChange" in input && input.metadataChange !== undefined
      ? patch(before?.metadata ?? {}, input.metadataChange, "metadata", options)
      : "metadata" in input
        ? (input.metadata ?? {})
        : (before?.metadata ?? {});
  if (!deleting) validatePropertyContracts(effective, properties, options.limits, fault);
  for (const { type: declaringType, side, constraint, address, actual } of endpointChecks) {
    if (actual !== undefined) {
      if (
        constraint.conformsTo.some(
          (required) => !actual.some((contract) => contract.descriptor.id === required),
        )
      )
        diagnosticFailure(
          [
            {
              type: declaringType,
              schemaLocation: `${declaringType}#/${side}/conformsTo`,
              message: "in-Scope endpoint fails an effective Type constraint",
            },
          ],
          options.limits,
        );
    } else {
      if (constraint.external === "none")
        diagnosticFailure(
          [
            {
              type: declaringType,
              schemaLocation: `${declaringType}#/${side}/external`,
              message: "external endpoint is forbidden by Type contract",
            },
          ],
          options.limits,
        );
      if (constraint.external === "bead" && !isExternalBead(address))
        diagnosticFailure(
          [
            {
              type: declaringType,
              schemaLocation: `${declaringType}#/${side}/external`,
              message: "external endpoint is not bead-shaped",
            },
          ],
          options.limits,
        );
    }
  }
  const sourceDescriptor = source
    ? contracts(source.type).find((entry) => entry.descriptor.id === source?.type)?.descriptor
    : undefined;
  const ownership =
    sourceDescriptor?.describes === "bead" ? sourceDescriptor.ownsOutgoing : undefined;
  const owned =
    ownership !== undefined && (Object.hasOwn(ownership, type) || Object.hasOwn(ownership, "*"));
  const noOp =
    !creating &&
    !deleting &&
    equal(properties, before?.properties) &&
    equal(commonMetadata, before?.metadata ?? {});
  const contextInput = "revisionProvenance" in input ? input.revisionProvenance : undefined;
  // Protocol defaults: omitted and {} both carry only undetermined states.
  const needsContext =
    !noOp &&
    (options.recordChangeContext ||
      (contextInput !== undefined &&
        (Object.hasOwn(contextInput, "agent") || Object.hasOwn(contextInput, "message"))));
  const metadata = (prior: ResourceRecord | undefined): VersionRecipe["metadata"] => {
    if (noOp && prior)
      return {
        context: { kind: "preserved", value: prior.revisionProvenance },
      };
    const actor = contextInput?.actor;
    return {
      ...(actor === undefined ? {} : { actor }),
      context: needsContext ? { kind: "native" } : { kind: "preserved", value: undefined },
    };
  };
  const revision = noOp ? before?.revision : deleting ? undefined : allocateRevision();
  let subjectRecipe: VersionRecipe | undefined = deleting
    ? undefined
    : {
        id: uri,
        type,
        revision: revision as string,
        properties,
        commonMetadata,
        metadata: metadata(before),
        endpoints,
      };
  const ownedLinks = (
    owner: VersionRecipe,
    descriptor: BeadTypeDescriptor,
    replacement?: VersionRecipe,
    removed?: string,
  ): VersionRecipe => {
    if (descriptor.ownsOutgoing === undefined) return owner;
    const entries: Record<string, PreparedLink[]> = Object.create(null) as Record<
      string,
      PreparedLink[]
    >;
    for (const name of Object.keys(descriptor.ownsOutgoing)) if (name !== "*") entries[name] = [];
    const live = tx
      .outgoingLinks(local(scope, owner.id) as string)
      .filter((link) => link.id !== removed && new URL(link.id, scope).href !== replacement?.id)
      .map(
        (link): PreparedLink => ({
          kind: "existing",
          value: storedRecord(link, scope) as LinkRecord,
        }),
      );
    if (replacement) live.push({ kind: "replacement", value: replacement });
    for (const prepared of live) {
      const link = prepared.value;
      if (Object.hasOwn(descriptor.ownsOutgoing, link.type) || descriptor.ownsOutgoing["*"]) {
        const bucket = entries[link.type] ?? [];
        entries[link.type] = bucket;
        bucket.push(prepared);
      }
    }
    for (const [name, links] of Object.entries(entries)) {
      links.sort((a, b) => compareCanonicalIds(a.value.id, b.value.id));
      const max = descriptor.ownsOutgoing[name]?.max;
      if (max !== undefined && links.length > max)
        diagnosticFailure(
          [
            {
              type: descriptor.id,
              schemaLocation: `${descriptor.id}#/ownsOutgoing/${name.replace(/~/g, "~0").replace(/\//g, "~1")}`,
              message: "owned Link set exceeds declared maximum",
            },
          ],
          options.limits,
        );
    }
    if (
      descriptor.ownsOutgoing["*"] &&
      Object.values(entries).reduce((count, links) => count + links.length, 0) >
        descriptor.ownsOutgoing["*"].max
    )
      diagnosticFailure(
        [
          {
            type: descriptor.id,
            schemaLocation: `${descriptor.id}#/ownsOutgoing/*`,
            message: "whole owned Link set exceeds wildcard maximum",
          },
        ],
        options.limits,
      );
    return { ...owner, ownedLinks: entries };
  };
  if (subjectRecipe && resourceKind === "bead" && declared.describes === "bead")
    subjectRecipe = ownedLinks(subjectRecipe, declared);
  let sourceRecipe: VersionRecipe | undefined;
  if (owned && source && sourceDescriptor?.describes === "bead") {
    const sourceRevision = noOp ? source.revision : allocateRevision();
    sourceRecipe = ownedLinks(
      {
        id: source.id,
        type: source.type,
        revision: sourceRevision,
        properties: source.properties,
        commonMetadata: source.metadata ?? {},
        metadata: metadata(source),
        endpoints: undefined,
      },
      sourceDescriptor,
      subjectRecipe,
      deleting ? id : undefined,
    );
  }
  if (!deleting && endpoints) {
    for (const policy of options.maximumEndpointMultiplicity) {
      if (!effective.some((entry) => entry.descriptor.id === policy.linkConformsTo)) continue;
      const address = referenceUri(endpoints[policy.endpoint]);
      const endpointId = local(scope, address);
      if (endpointId === undefined) continue;
      let count = 1;
      for (const link of tx.incidentLinks(endpointId)) {
        if (link.id === id || link[policy.endpoint] !== endpointId) continue;
        const record = storedRecord(link, scope);
        if (contracts(record.type).some((entry) => entry.descriptor.id === policy.linkConformsTo))
          count++;
      }
      if (count > policy.max) fail("aggregate-constraint-violation");
    }
  }
  // Preparation has made no graph writes. Recipes are private, never provisional
  // Resource records passed to policy/storage. Their inventory checks ran once.
  if (subjectRecipe) freeze(subjectRecipe);
  if (sourceRecipe) freeze(sourceRecipe);
  // C is a conditional logical commit point only under the owner's exclusive
  // single-connection, synchronous, immutable-snapshot contract. Final checks
  // below may still refuse; only the outer S6 COMMIT permits a success to escape.
  // Adding concurrent readers/WAL, async or reentrant owners reopens this proof.
  const context =
    subjectRecipe?.metadata.context.kind === "native" ||
    sourceRecipe?.metadata.context.kind === "native"
      ? makeContext(contextInput, observeCommitTime(options.observeCommitTime))
      : undefined;
  const materialized = new Map<VersionRecipe, ResourceRecord>();
  const after = subjectRecipe
    ? materializeVersion(subjectRecipe, context, materialized)
    : undefined;
  const sourceAfter = sourceRecipe
    ? (materializeVersion(sourceRecipe, context, materialized) as BeadRecord)
    : undefined;
  if (
    creating
      ? !after || !options.policy.canCreate(after)
      : !before || !options.policy.canWrite(before, after)
  )
    fail("forbidden");
  if (source && sourceAfter && !options.policy.canWrite(source, sourceAfter)) fail("forbidden");
  if (
    "expectedRevision" in input &&
    input.expectedRevision !== undefined &&
    input.expectedRevision !== before?.revision
  )
    fail("revision-mismatch");
  if (deleting && resourceKind === "bead" && tx.incidentLinks(id).length)
    fail("incident-links-exist");
  if ("propertiesChange" in input || "metadataChange" in input) {
    const limits = options.limits;
    const changes = [
      ...("propertiesChange" in input ? (input.propertiesChange ?? []) : []),
      ...("metadataChange" in input ? (input.metadataChange ?? []) : []),
    ];
    if (limits.patchOperations !== undefined && changes.length > limits.patchOperations)
      fail("limit-exceeded");
    for (const change of changes)
      if (
        (limits.patchPathBytes !== undefined &&
          Buffer.byteLength(change.path) > limits.patchPathBytes) ||
        (limits.patchPathDepth !== undefined &&
          (change.path === "" ? 0 : change.path.split("/").length - 1) > limits.patchPathDepth)
      )
        fail("limit-exceeded");
  }
  const writes: EvaluatorStoredResource[] = [];
  for (const record of [after, sourceAfter]) {
    if (!record) continue;
    const bodyJson = stringifyJsonValue(record);
    if (
      (options.limits.propertiesBytes !== undefined &&
        Buffer.byteLength(stringifyJsonValue(record.properties)) >
          options.limits.propertiesBytes) ||
      (options.limits.representationBytes !== undefined &&
        Buffer.byteLength(bodyJson) > options.limits.representationBytes)
    )
      fail("limit-exceeded");
    if (!noOp) {
      const stored = { id: local(scope, record.id) as string, bodyJson };
      writes.push(
        "source" in record
          ? {
              ...stored,
              kind: "link",
              source: local(scope, referenceUri(record.source)) ?? referenceUri(record.source),
              target: local(scope, referenceUri(record.target)) ?? referenceUri(record.target),
            }
          : { ...stored, kind: "bead" },
      );
    }
  }
  const outcome: ReadUpdateMutationResult = deleting
    ? {
        outcome: "deleted",
        deleted: {
          resourceKind,
          resource: {
            id: before?.id as string,
            type: before?.type as string,
            revision: before?.revision as string,
          },
        },
        ...(sourceAfter ? { source: sourceAfter.id, sourceRevision: sourceAfter.revision } : {}),
      }
    : {
        outcome: creating ? "created" : "updated",
        resource: after as ResourceRecord,
        ...(sourceAfter ? { source: sourceAfter.id, sourceRevision: sourceAfter.revision } : {}),
      };
  // No Resource write happens until every validation/policy/CAS/limit check passes.
  fault.check();
  for (const write of writes) tx.putResource(write);
  if (deleting) tx.deleteResource(id);
  return freeze({
    effect: "success",
    outcome,
    resolutions,
    changed: writes.map((write) => write.id),
    deleted: deleting ? [id] : [],
  });
}
/** Private version recipes cannot be supplied to a Resource consumer. Only the
 * new Link recipe may be referenced by its source; old Links stay whole records.
 */
interface VersionRecipe {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly type: string;
  readonly revision: string;
  readonly properties: BeadRecord["properties"];
  readonly commonMetadata: NonNullable<BeadRecord["metadata"]>;
  readonly endpoints: { readonly source: Reference; readonly target: Reference } | undefined;
  readonly metadata: {
    readonly actor?: RevisionActor;
    readonly context:
      | { readonly kind: "native" }
      | { readonly kind: "preserved"; readonly value: RevisionProvenance | undefined };
  };
  readonly ownedLinks?: Readonly<Record<string, readonly PreparedLink[]>>;
}
type PreparedLink =
  | { readonly kind: "existing"; readonly value: LinkRecord }
  | { readonly kind: "replacement"; readonly value: VersionRecipe };


===== docs/specs/bdp.md whole-file SHA256 f036103ffdf5f89fa3a2033570e1e987c4cd203cd80b1c633cb44615123f892e explicit excerpt lines 1880-1915 =====
or advertise generic BDP replication. Generic pre-removal administrative reports,
preview endpoints, minimum retention promises, exact-byte witnesses, sync hints,
extra scheme mappings, erased-row enumeration, bulk checks and the alternative
surviving-citation lifecycle remain deferred. Local policy/tooling is allowed;
none weakens required Gone evidence, erasure or incident-Link refusal.

### Problem details

Except for the `405` method rejections and unexpected internal `500`
responses defined below, every unsuccessful BDP response uses RFC 9457
Problem Details. BDP defines a small set of stable problem-type families. The
required `code` member identifies the exact normative condition within its
family. Each code fixes its HTTP status and retry disposition. The Read
profile uses this closed table:

| Code | Family suffix | HTTP status | Retry |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `malformed-request` | `request` | 400 | `never` |
| `invalid-parameter` | `request` | 400 | `never` |
| `unauthenticated` | `authentication` | 401 | `after-state-change` |
| `forbidden` | `authorization` | 403 | `after-state-change` |
| `resource-not-found` | `not-found` | 404 | `after-state-change` |
| `resource-pruned` | `gone` | 410 | `never` |
| `resource-erased` | `gone` | 410 | `never` |
| `foreign-view` | `conflict` | 409 | `after-state-change` |
| `cursor-expired` | `gone` | 410 | `after-state-change` |
| `request-too-large` | `size` | 413 | `never` |
| `limit-exceeded` | `size` | 413 | `never` |
| `rate-limited` | `rate-limit` | 429 | `after-delay` |
| `temporarily-unavailable` | `unavailable` | 503 | `after-delay` |
| `revision-unknown` | `not-found` | 404 | `after-state-change` |
| `revision-unretained` | `conflict` | 409 | `after-state-change` |
| `revision-reorganized` | `gone` | 410 | `after-state-change` |
| `revision-not-tracked` | `conflict` | 409 | `after-state-change` |
| `revision-unrepresentable` | `conflict` | 409 | `after-state-change` |


===== docs/specs/bdp.md whole-file SHA256 f036103ffdf5f89fa3a2033570e1e987c4cd203cd80b1c633cb44615123f892e explicit excerpt lines 2700-2775 =====

Omission of a mutation bound does not permit partial mutation, silent
truncation or a non-normative failure response.

- `patch.operations` bounds the combined operation count of `propertiesChange`
  and `metadataChange` in one Resource update; `patch.pathBytes` and
  `patch.pathDepth` bound each path in either patch;
- `sequence.operations` bounds members in one Read+Update sequence;
- `validation.diagnostics` counts entries in, and `validation.diagnosticBytes`
  counts UTF-8 bytes of, the serialized `diagnostics` list a
  `validation-failed` problem carries under
  [Read+Update problem details](#readupdate-problem-details); the group is mutation surface: it
  is not advertised by a Read discovery document, and a Read+Update authority that omits diagnostics beyond a bound MUST
  advertise it;
- `retention.idempotency` is the minimum interval for which an authority retains
  an idempotency-key disposition after its terminal outcome, under
  [Outcome retention](#outcome-retention).

### Read+Update sequence target

The Read+Update profile exposes `operations/sequence` as a
convenience carrier for the six single-Resource operations and the two alias
operations under [Alias targets](#alias-targets). A sequence is ordered and partially committing.

A sequence contains one or more operation members. Each member carries its own
`idempotencyKey` and one of the eight singleton operation records: the six
Resource records, or the two alias records. The authority
validates the carrier and operation-record syntax before starting, claims
every member's key in declaration order under
[Duplicate keys and retained dispositions](#duplicate-keys-and-retained-dispositions),
and then:

1. starts members strictly in declaration order and never runs them in
   parallel;
2. gives each member an individually atomic terminal outcome before starting
   the next;
3. commits each successful member immediately and never rolls it back because
   a later member fails;
4. continues after a failed member so later independent work can run; and
5. permits unrelated requests to interleave between members, taking no
   sequence-wide transaction, reservation, or lock.

A create member may supply `name`, using
`[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]*` syntax. A later member
may use `@name` wherever the created Resource's ID of that kind is accepted.
The binding becomes available only after the create commits — or, on a
retry, when the creating member's retained or expired disposition supplies
the identity it allocated. A reference that is forward, unknown, or of the
wrong Resource kind is decidable from the request text and is rejected
before execution. A reference to a creating member whose
retained disposition is a failure fails that member permanently; a
reference to a creating member whose disposition in the same request was
transient fails that member transiently, under the envelope rules below.
Neither prevents later independent members from running. Bindings are
confined to one sequence request and do not add isolation: an interleaving
request may update or delete the committed Resource before a later member
uses it.

The response preserves declaration order and contains one terminal result or
problem for every member. A syntactically admitted sequence returns `200 OK`
even when some members fail; the per-member dispositions carry partial
success. A carrier or operation-record syntax error is rejected before
execution with a direct problem response. Sequence responses are inline results. Retrying a member with the same idempotency key and
semantic operation returns its retained disposition. Using that key for
different semantics is an idempotency conflict.

The sequence carrier itself does not use an `Idempotency-Key` HTTP field;
its member keys are authoritative, and a sequence request that carries the
field is rejected before execution with `malformed-request`. The envelopes,
key rules, duplicate handling, and retention rules in the subsections below
complete the carrier. The resolved decisions and their alternatives are recorded in
`docs/design/w1-read-update-decisions.md`. Implementations use the wire
contracts here; the decision history supplies no alternate wire behavior.

#### Sequence request envelope


===== docs/specs/bdp.md whole-file SHA256 f036103ffdf5f89fa3a2033570e1e987c4cd203cd80b1c633cb44615123f892e explicit excerpt lines 4045-4090 =====
is not; the authority MAY retain it longer. While it is retained, a retry
of the failed transaction returns that same failed receipt. Once the
authority forgets it, its URL answers the uniform `404` as a retracted
receipt's does, and its key is unknown: a later presentation executes as a
new mutation under the guards the request carries — a first execution,
since the failed transaction committed nothing and allocated nothing
durable. A failed receipt never enters `detail` `expired`; that state
belongs to completed receipts. Both profiles therefore keep tombstones for
committed effects only, as [Outcome retention](#outcome-retention) does. A
client that has refreshed its state constructs a new request under a new
key.

On a Transactional Scope the `sequence` target is the carrier defined under
[Read+Update sequence target](#readupdate-sequence-target), and it keeps
its envelope, its member records, its declaration order, its separate
commitment, its lack of isolation, and its `@name` rules unchanged; what
the profile changes is what a member's disposition is. Each member is a
one-operation Mutation Transaction: the sequence's admission admits every
member whose key is unknown, in declaration order, recording a pending
receipt for each — the profile's form of claiming a key — and the members
then execute in order, each committing its own state, change group, and
terminal receipt. The envelope projects each member's receipt into one
entry, and it adds no receipt member. The bundle's
`transactionalSequenceResponse` specializes the shared envelope with
`transactionalSequenceMemberProblem`: its `allocated` extension, permitted
only on `idempotency-expired`, is `sequenceAllocatedIdentity`: exactly
`{ id, type }` when the identity is disclosed or `{ withheld: true }` when
it is withheld, distinct from a receipt's indexed `allocated` array.
A completed Resource creation's expired projection MUST carry exactly one of
these forms; every other operation's expired projection MUST omit `allocated`. Here a Resource creation is an operation that
allocated a Resource identity. An alias put is not one even when its alias
result says `created`; its expired sequence projection omits `allocated`.
`resource-erased` member problems reject
`pointer` just as direct problems do.
A member newly admitted by this carrier executes under its own pending
receipt and exclusive ownership; the in-flight projection below applies only
to an execution this carrier did not admit. The unresolved admission and
direct-carrier comparison rules above preserve this ownership distinction. The projections
are:

- a `completed` receipt with its detail available projects the receipt's
  one result entry in the shape of
  [Sequence response envelope](#sequence-response-envelope), carrying
  `operationIndex` and `operationName` for the present member; an entry
  the current view withholds projects as the `forbidden` member problem of
  [Duplicate keys and retained dispositions](#duplicate-keys-and-retained-dispositions),

    entry: string,

  process.env.BDP_TRANSACTIONAL_INSTALLED_ENTRY ??
  path.join(root, "packages/server/dist/transaction-installed-process.js");

function artifacts() {
  const task = `${scope}types/task`,
    link = `${scope}types/relationship`,
    propertiesSchema = `${scope}schemas/properties`;
  const descriptors = [
    { id: task, name: "Task", describes: "bead", conformsTo: [], propertiesSchema },
    {
      id: link,
      name: "Relationship",
      describes: "link",
      conformsTo: [],
      propertiesSchema,
      source: { conformsTo: [task], external: "opaque" },
      target: { conformsTo: [task], external: "opaque" },
    },
  ];
  return {
    descriptors: descriptors.map((d) => ({ retrievalUri: d.id, utf8: JSON.stringify(d) })),
    schemas: [
      {
        retrievalUri: propertiesSchema,
        utf8: JSON.stringify({
          $id: propertiesSchema,
          type: "object",
          properties: { title: { type: "string" } },
          additionalProperties: false,
        }),
      },
    ],
  };
}
const sourceCommit =
  process.env.BDP_TRANSACTIONAL_SOURCE_COMMIT ??
  execFileSync("git", ["rev-parse", "HEAD"], { cwd: root, encoding: "utf8" }).trim();
function errorCauses(error: unknown): Set<unknown> {
  const seen = new Set<unknown>();
  const visit = (value: unknown) => {
    if (seen.has(value)) return;
    seen.add(value);
    if (value instanceof AggregateError) for (const cause of value.errors) visit(cause);
    if (value instanceof Error && value.cause !== undefined) visit(value.cause);
  };
  visit(error);
  return seen;
}
type InstalledObservation = {
  result: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof runCumulativeTransactionalMatrix>>;
  sessions: Record<string, unknown>[];
  baseline: ReturnType<typeof installedTransactionBinding>;
};
async function observe(control?: {
  readonly budgetMs?: number;
  /** Negative-test deadline begins after the first real exchange, with startup still bounded. */
  readonly afterFirstExchangeBudgetMs?: number;
  readonly scenarioSelection?: readonly string[];
  /** Retain genuine returned evidence before cleanup can fail; never replace it. */
  readonly onObserved?: (observed: InstalledObservation) => void;
  readonly invalidReceiptCredentials?: boolean | "set-update" | "set-delete";
  /** Negative-only removal of one already declared canonical set route. */
  readonly omitRoute?: {
    readonly method: "POST";
    readonly url: `${typeof scope}operations/${"update-where" | "delete-where"}`;
  };
  readonly signal?: AbortSignal;
  readonly children?: ChildProcess[];
  /** Negative-test gate after actual ordinary wire, never a response replacement. */
  readonly afterExchange?: (response: HttpExchangeResponse, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<void>;
  readonly ready?: (ready: Record<string, unknown>) => Record<string, unknown>;
  readonly manifest?: (value: {
    scenarios: { id: string; actions: Record<string, unknown>[] }[];
  }) => void;
}) {
  const budgetMs = control?.budgetMs ?? 90000;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(budgetMs) || budgetMs < 1 || budgetMs > 90000)
    throw new Error("invalid cohort budget");
  const afterFirstExchangeBudgetMs = control?.afterFirstExchangeBudgetMs;
  if (
    afterFirstExchangeBudgetMs !== undefined &&
    (!Number.isSafeInteger(afterFirstExchangeBudgetMs) ||
      afterFirstExchangeBudgetMs < 1 ||
      afterFirstExchangeBudgetMs > budgetMs)
  )
    throw new Error("invalid after-first-exchange cohort budget");
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const signal = control?.signal
    ? AbortSignal.any([control.signal, controller.signal])
    : controller.signal;
  signal.throwIfAborted();
  const changed = JSON.parse(manifestSource.bytes.toString()) as {
    scenarios: { id: string; actions: Record<string, unknown>[] }[];
  };
  control?.manifest?.(changed);
  const runBundle = control?.manifest
    ? createCumulativeTransactionalArtifactBundle(loadProfileContractSelection("transactional"), {
        fixture: fixtureSource,
        manifest: { bytes: Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(changed)) },
      })
    : bundle;
  const directory = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), "bdp-tx-installed-"));
  const operator = randomBytes(32).toString("base64url"),
    reader = randomBytes(32).toString("base64url");
  const configuration = {
    scope,
    directory: path.join(directory, "bootstrap"),
    create: true,
    credentials: [
      { token: operator, principal: "same", canMutate: true },
      { token: reader, principal: "same", canMutate: false },
    ],
    artifacts: artifacts(),
  };
  let target: ReturnType<typeof createTransactionalExecutionBinding> | undefined;
  const sessions: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
  const deniedReceiptUrls = new Set<string>();
  const expireCohort = () => controller.abort(new Error("installed cohort deadline"));
  const timer = setTimeout(expireCohort, budgetMs);
  let firstExchangeTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  let observed: InstalledObservation | undefined;
  let failure: unknown;
  const cleanupFailures: unknown[] = [];
  try {
    const bootstrap = await launchInstalledTransaction(entry, configuration, signal);
    control?.children?.push(bootstrap.child);
    let baseline: ReturnType<typeof installedTransactionBinding>;
    try {
      baseline = installedTransactionBinding(bootstrap.ready, sourceCommit, fixtureSource);
    } finally {
      await bootstrap.close();
    }
    const routes = bundle.manifest.scenarios
      .flatMap((s) => s.actions ?? [])
      .flatMap((a) => {
        if (a.family !== "http") return [];
        const base =
          a.target.binding === "scope" ? scope : bundle.fixture.bindings?.[a.target.binding];
        if (!base) throw new Error("predeclared target unavailable");
        const url = new URL(a.target.path ?? "", base);
        for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(a.target.query ?? {})) {
          if (typeof value !== "string") throw new Error("literal query required");
          url.searchParams.set(key, value);
        }
        return [{ method: a.method, url: url.href }];
      });
    const uniqueRoutes = routes.filter(
      (r, i) => routes.findIndex((o) => o.url === r.url && o.method === r.method) === i,
    );
    // Independently authored route inventory, not a second derivation from the manifest.
    expect(uniqueRoutes).toEqual([
      { method: "POST", url: `${scope}operations/create-bead` },
      { method: "POST", url: `${scope}operations/batch` },
      { method: "POST", url: `${scope}operations/sequence` },
      { method: "POST", url: `${scope}operations/update-bead` },
      { method: "GET", url: `${scope}beads/?limit=32` },
      { method: "GET", url: `${scope}events/` },
      { method: "GET", url: `${scope}beads/a` },
      { method: "GET", url: `${scope}links/l` },
      { method: "GET", url: `${scope}beads/new` },
      { method: "GET", url: `${scope}changes/?after=genesis` },
      { method: "GET", url: `${scope}links/?limit=32` },
      { method: "GET", url: `${scope}beads/b` },
      { method: "GET", url: `${scope}beads/c` },
      { method: "POST", url: `${scope}operations/update-where` },
      { method: "POST", url: `${scope}operations/delete-where` },
    ]);
    const omitted = control?.omitRoute;
    if (omitted) {
      expect([`${scope}operations/update-where`, `${scope}operations/delete-where`]).toContain(
        omitted.url,
      );
      expect(omitted.method).toBe("POST");
      expect(
        uniqueRoutes.filter((r) => r.url === omitted.url && r.method === omitted.method),
      ).toHaveLength(1);
    }
    const exactMode: ExactHttpModeOptions = {
      scope,
      profile: "transactional",
      routes: uniqueRoutes.filter(
        (r) => !omitted || r.url !== omitted.url || r.method !== omitted.method,
      ),
      maximumRequestHeaderBytes: 16384,
      maximumRequestBodyBytes: 65536,
      defaultCredentialRef: "operator",
      credentialHandles: [
        { id: "operator", headerNames: ["authorization"] },
        { id: "reader", headerNames: ["authorization"] },
        { id: "anonymous", headerNames: [] },
      ],
      resolveCredentials: ({ credentialRef, url }) =>
        credentialRef === "anonymous"
          ? {}
          : {
              authorization: `Bearer ${(control?.invalidReceiptCredentials === true && url.startsWith(`${scope}receipts/`)) || deniedReceiptUrls.has(url) ? "deliberately-unknown" : credentialRef === "operator" ? operator : reader}`,
            },
    };
    target = createTransactionalExecutionBinding({
      baseline,
      sourceCommit,
      fixtureSource,
      exactMode,
      launch: async (scenario, reopen, signal) => {
        if (signal.aborted) throw new Error("launch aborted");
        const child = await launchInstalledTransaction(
          entry,
          {
            ...configuration,
            directory: path.join(directory, scenario),
            create: !reopen,
          },
          signal,
        );
        control?.children?.push(child.child);
        sessions.push({ ...child.ready, scenario, reopen });
        return control?.ready ? { ...child, ready: control.ready(child.ready) } : child;
      },
    });
    const result = await runCumulativeTransactionalMatrix(
      {
        scope,
        signal,
        profile: "transactional",
        seed: 0,
        artifactBundle: runBundle,
        ...(control?.scenarioSelection === undefined
          ? {}
          : { scenarioSelection: control.scenarioSelection }),
        execute: async () => {
          throw new Error("generic HTTP executor forbidden");
        },
        exactExecution: {
          execute: async (request) => {
            const response = await target!.target.execute(request);
            if (afterFirstExchangeBudgetMs !== undefined && firstExchangeTimer === undefined) {
              firstExchangeTimer = setTimeout(expireCohort, afterFirstExchangeBudgetMs);
            }
            if (
              typeof control?.invalidReceiptCredentials === "string" &&
              response.status === 200 &&
              [
                baseline.operationTargets.updateWhere,
                baseline.operationTargets.deleteWhere,
              ].includes(response.url)
            ) {
              const receipt = JSON.parse(response.bodyText) as {
                id?: unknown;
                idempotencyKey?: unknown;
              };
              if (
                receipt.idempotencyKey === control.invalidReceiptCredentials &&
                typeof receipt.id === "string"
              )
                deniedReceiptUrls.add(receipt.id);
            }
            await control?.afterExchange?.(response, signal);
            signal.throwIfAborted();
            return response;
          },
          configuration: target.target.exactConfiguration!,
        },
        harness: {
          ...target.target.harness,
          cleanup: async (...args) => {
            try {
              await target!.target.harness.cleanup(...args);
            } catch (error) {
              cleanupFailures.push(error);
              throw error;
            }
          },
        },
        schemaValidator: createJsonSchemaValidator(schema),
        declaredTargetLabel: "private-installed-transactional-partial",
        prepareTimeoutMs: 10000,
        cleanupTimeoutMs: 10000,
        requestTimeoutMs: 5000,
      },
      target.context,
    );
    observed = { result, sessions, baseline };
    control?.onObserved?.(observed);
  } catch (error) {
    failure = error;
  } finally {
    // The runner can report abort as row state; retain the cohort's original
    // reason before a subsequent child/session cleanup failure is aggregated.
    if (failure === undefined && signal.aborted) failure = signal.reason;
    try {
      await target?.target.close();
    } catch (cleanup) {
      cleanupFailures.push(cleanup);
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      clearTimeout(firstExchangeTimer);
      rmSync(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  }
  if (cleanupFailures.length) {
    const causes = [...new Set([...(failure === undefined ? [] : [failure]), ...cleanupFailures])];
    throw new AggregateError(causes, "installed cohort cleanup failed");
  }
  if (failure !== undefined) throw failure;
  signal.throwIfAborted();
  if (!observed) throw new Error("missing installed observation");
  return observed;
}
